{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01E6tCaDsUjpkBQy2AB5jfSs","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→```markdown\n     2→# UtilityChain Core Suite – Architecture Guide\n     3→_Blockchain & DeFi Utility Framework_  \n     4→Version: **1.0**  \n     5→Target Framework: **.NET 8**  \n     6→Author: Core Engineering Team\n     7→\n     8→---\n     9→\n    10→## 1. Design Philosophy\n    11→UtilityChain is a **monolithic executable** that embraces modular design through\n    12→well-defined internal contracts (interfaces) and **in-process** event dispatching.\n    13→This hybrid approach provides:\n    14→- **Single-binary deployment** – zero external dependencies beyond .NET 8.\n    15→- **Zero-copy data-flow** – shared memory models outperform gRPC/HTTP hops.\n    16→- **Deterministic upgrades** – state migrations occur within the same address\n    17→  space, eliminating cross-service version skews.\n    18→- **Pluggable subsystems** – `IConsensusEngine`, `IGovernancePolicy`,\n    19→  `ITokenStandard`, etc., are hot-swappable via the Factory/Strategy pattern.\n    20→\n    21→> **NOTE:** The project intentionally **does not** target public-chain\n    22→> interoperability. Its primary audience is private or consortium deployments\n    23→> that value operational ownership over maximal decentralization.\n    24→\n    25→---\n    26→\n    27→## 2. High-Level Component Map\n    28→\n    29→```mermaid\n    30→flowchart LR\n    31→    subgraph CLI & UI\n    32→        CLI[Command Line Tooling]\n    33→        WalletDesktop[Desktop Wallet]\n    34→        IDE[Smart-Contract IDE]\n    35→    end\n    36→    subgraph API Gateway\n    37→        REST[REST Controller]\n    38→        GraphQL[GraphQL Controller]\n    39→    end\n    40→    subgraph Core Engine\n    41→        EventBus\n    42→        StateMgr[State Machine Coordinator]\n    43→        Ledger[UTXO / Account Ledger]\n    44→        SmartVM[Smart-Contract VM]\n    45→        NFT[NFT Toolkit]\n    46→        Token[Token Toolkit]\n    47→        Consensus[Consensus Engines]\n    48→        Staking[Staking Module]\n    49→        Governance[Governance Module]\n    50→    end\n    51→    subgraph Persist\n    52→        RocksDb[(RocksDB)]\n    53→        Snapshot[(Snapshot & Backup)]\n    54→    end\n    55→    CLI & UI -->|IPC / Localhost| API Gateway\n    56→    API Gateway --> EventBus\n    57→    EventBus --> StateMgr\n    58→    StateMgr --> Ledger\n    59→    StateMgr --> SmartVM\n    60→    SmartVM --> Ledger\n    61→    Staking --> Ledger\n    62→    Consensus --> Ledger\n    63→    Consensus --> EventBus\n    64→    NFT --> SmartVM\n    65→    Token --> SmartVM\n    66→    Governance --> EventBus\n    67→    Ledger --> RocksDb\n    68→    Snapshot --> RocksDb\n    69→```\n    70→\n    71→---\n    72→\n    73→## 3. Core Architectural Patterns\n    74→\n    75→| Pattern             | Purpose                                                           |\n    76→|---------------------|-------------------------------------------------------------------|\n    77→| **Event Driven**    | Decouples producers/consumers (`EventBus`)                        |\n    78→| **State Machine**   | Linearizes multi-module state mutations (`StateCoordinator`)      |\n    79→| **Proxy**           | Lazy-loads external cryptography providers                        |\n    80→| **Strategy**        | Hot-swappable consensus & staking algorithms                      |\n    81→| **Factory**         | Creates domain objects (`Block`, `Transaction`, `Token`)          |\n    82→| **Observer**        | Real-time UI updates & monitoring                                 |\n    83→\n    84→---\n    85→\n    86→## 4. Module Specifications\n    87→\n    88→### 4.1 EventBus\n    89→\n    90→```csharp\n    91→public interface IEventBus\n    92→{\n    93→    void Publish<TEvent>(TEvent @event) where TEvent : DomainEvent;\n    94→    IDisposable Subscribe<TEvent>(Action<TEvent> handler) where TEvent : DomainEvent;\n    95→}\n    96→```\n    97→\n    98→Implementation wraps a lock-free,\n    99→multi-producer → multi-consumer ring buffer (powered by\n   100→`System.Threading.Channels`) facilitating up to **8 M msgs/sec** on commodity\n   101→hardware.\n   102→\n   103→### 4.2 State Machine Coordinator\n   104→\n   105→Responsible for **serializing side-effect-ful transitions** (block addition,\n   106→governance rule updates, staking rewards). Incoming transactions are first\n   107→validated in parallel; committing them is single-threaded to guarantee a total\n   108→order.\n   109→\n   110→```csharp\n   111→internal sealed class StateCoordinator : IAsyncDisposable\n   112→{\n   113→    private readonly Channel<IStateCommand> _channel =\n   114→        Channel.CreateUnbounded<IStateCommand>(new UnboundedChannelOptions {\n   115→            SingleReader = true,\n   116→            SingleWriter = false\n   117→        });\n   118→\n   119→    public async ValueTask<IStateResult> EnqueueAsync(IStateCommand cmd,\n   120→                                                      CancellationToken ct = default)\n   121→    {\n   122→        await _channel.Writer.WriteAsync(cmd, ct);\n   123→        return await cmd.Completion.Task.ConfigureAwait(false);\n   124→    }\n   125→\n   126→    // Single reader task\n   127→    private async Task RunAsync()\n   128→    {\n   129→        await foreach (var cmd in _channel.Reader.ReadAllAsync())\n   130→        {\n   131→            IStateResult result = await cmd.ExecuteAsync();\n   132→            cmd.Completion.TrySetResult(result);\n   133→        }\n   134→    }\n   135→}\n   136→```\n   137→\n   138→### 4.3 Consensus Engine Strategy\n   139→\n   140→`ConsensusEngineFactory` selects a concrete engine at startup\n   141→(`ProofOfAuthority`, `DPoS`, or `RaftBFT`) based on `appsettings.json` or CLI\n   142→flag.\n   143→\n   144→```csharp\n   145→public interface IConsensusEngine\n   146→{\n   147→    string Name { get; }\n   148→    ValueTask<ConsensusResult> ValidateBlockAsync(Block block, CancellationToken ct);\n   149→    ValueTask<ConsensusResult> FinalizeBlockAsync(Block block, CancellationToken ct);\n   150→}\n   151→```\n   152→\n   153→---\n   154→\n   155→## 5. Data Persistence\n   156→\n   157→1. **RocksDB** key-value store  \n   158→   - Column Families: `blocks`, `tx_index`, `state`, `snapshots`\n   159→2. **Snapshot Service**  \n   160→   - Creates LZ4-compressed snapshots every _N_ blocks.\n   161→   - Uses Copy-on-Write to avoid write stalls.\n   162→\n   163→---\n   164→\n   165→## 6. Security Model\n   166→\n   167→- **Memory-hard KDF** (`Argon2id`) for wallet key encryption.\n   168→- **Constant-time cryptographic operations** to prevent side-channel attacks.\n   169→- **Capability-based sandbox** for Smart-Contract VM; contracts must declare\n   170→  required capabilities (`storage.read`, `storage.write`, `token.mint`).\n   171→\n   172→---\n   173→\n   174→## 7. Extensibility Guidelines\n   175→\n   176→1. Implement the respective interface (`IConsensusEngine`, `IStakingCalculator`,\n   177→   etc.).\n   178→2. Register your type in `module.json`:\n   179→\n   180→```json\n   181→{\n   182→  \"consensus\": \"MyCompany.UtilityChain.PowEngine, MyCompany.UtilityChain\"\n   183→}\n   184→```\n   185→\n   186→3. Design Philosophy\nUtilityChain is a **monolithic executable** that embraces modular design through\nwell-defined internal contracts (interfaces) and **in-process** event dispatching.\nThis hybrid approach provides:\n- **Single-binary deployment** – zero external dependencies beyond .NET 8.\n- **Zero-copy data-flow** – shared memory models outperform gRPC/HTTP hops.\n- **Deterministic upgrades** – state migrations occur within the same address\n  space, eliminating cross-service version skews.\n- **Pluggable subsystems** – `IConsensusEngine`, `IGovernancePolicy`,\n  `ITokenStandard`, etc., are hot-swappable via the Factory/Strategy pattern.\n\n> **NOTE:** The project intentionally **does not** target public-chain\n> interoperability. Its primary audience is private or consortium deployments\n> that value operational ownership over maximal decentralization.\n\n---\n\n## 2. High-Level Component Map\n\n```mermaid\nflowchart LR\n    subgraph CLI & UI\n        CLI[Command Line Tooling]\n        WalletDesktop[Desktop Wallet]\n        IDE[Smart-Contract IDE]\n    end\n    subgraph API Gateway\n        REST[REST Controller]\n        GraphQL[GraphQL Controller]\n    end\n    subgraph Core Engine\n        EventBus\n        StateMgr[State Machine Coordinator]\n        Ledger[UTXO / Account Ledger]\n        SmartVM[Smart-Contract VM]\n        NFT[NFT Toolkit]\n        Token[Token Toolkit]\n        Consensus[Consensus Engines]\n        Staking[Staking Module]\n        Governance[Governance Module]\n    end\n    subgraph Persist\n        RocksDb[(RocksDB)]\n        Snapshot[(Snapshot & Backup)]\n    end\n    CLI & UI -->|IPC / Localhost| API Gateway\n    API Gateway --> EventBus\n    EventBus --> StateMgr\n    StateMgr --> Ledger\n    StateMgr --> SmartVM\n    SmartVM --> Ledger\n    Staking --> Ledger\n    Consensus --> Ledger\n    Consensus --> EventBus\n    NFT --> SmartVM\n    Token --> SmartVM\n    Governance --> EventBus\n    Ledger --> RocksDb\n    Snapshot --> RocksDb\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. Core Architectural Patterns\n\n| Pattern             | Purpose                                                           |\n|---------------------|-------------------------------------------------------------------|\n| **Event Driven**    | Decouples producers/consumers (`EventBus`)                        |\n| **State Machine**   | Linearizes multi-module state mutations (`StateCoordinator`)      |\n| **Proxy**           | Lazy-loads external cryptography providers                        |\n| **Strategy**        | Hot-swappable consensus & staking algorithms                      |\n| **Factory**         | Creates domain objects (`Block`, `Transaction`, `Token`)          |\n| **Observer**        | Real-time UI updates & monitoring                                 |\n\n---\n\n## 4. Module Specifications\n\n### 4.1 EventBus\n\n```csharp\npublic interface IEventBus\n{\n    void Publish<TEvent>(TEvent @event) where TEvent : DomainEvent;\n    IDisposable Subscribe<TEvent>(Action<TEvent> handler) where TEvent : DomainEvent;\n}\n```\n\nImplementation wraps a lock-free,\nmulti-producer → multi-consumer ring buffer (powered by\n`System.Threading.Channels`) facilitating up to **8 M msgs/sec** on commodity\nhardware.\n\n### 4.2 State Machine Coordinator\n\nResponsible for **serializing side-effect-ful transitions** (block addition,\ngovernance rule updates, staking rewards). Incoming transactions are first\nvalidated in parallel; committing them is single-threaded to guarantee a total\norder.\n\n```csharp\ninternal sealed class StateCoordinator : IAsyncDisposable\n{\n    private readonly Channel<IStateCommand> _channel =\n        Channel.CreateUnbounded<IStateCommand>(new UnboundedChannelOptions {\n            SingleReader = true,\n            SingleWriter = false\n        });\n\n    public async ValueTask<IStateResult> EnqueueAsync(IStateCommand cmd,\n                                                      CancellationToken ct = default)\n    {\n        await _channel.Writer.WriteAsync(cmd, ct);\n        return await cmd.Completion.Task.ConfigureAwait(false);\n    }\n\n    // Single reader task\n    private async Task RunAsync()\n    {\n        await foreach (var cmd in _channel.Reader.ReadAllAsync())\n        {\n            IStateResult result = await cmd.ExecuteAsync();\n            cmd.Completion.TrySetResult(result);\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 4.3 Consensus Engine Strategy\n\n`ConsensusEngineFactory` selects a concrete engine at startup\n(`ProofOfAuthority`, `DPoS`, or `RaftBFT`) based on `appsettings.json` or CLI\nflag.\n\n```csharp\npublic interface IConsensusEngine\n{\n    string Name { get; }\n    ValueTask<ConsensusResult> ValidateBlockAsync(Block block, CancellationToken ct);\n    ValueTask<ConsensusResult> FinalizeBlockAsync(Block block, CancellationToken ct);\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Data Persistence\n\n1. **RocksDB** key-value store  \n   - Column Families: `blocks`, `tx_index`, `state`, `snapshots`\n2. **Snapshot Service**  \n   - Creates LZ4-compressed snapshots every _N_ blocks.\n   - Uses Copy-on-Write to avoid write stalls.\n\n---\n\n## 6. Security Model\n\n- **Memory-hard KDF** (`Argon2id`) for wallet key encryption.\n- **Constant-time cryptographic operations** to prevent side-channel attacks.\n- **Capability-based sandbox** for Smart-Contract VM; contracts must declare\n  required capabilities (`storage.read`, `storage.write`, `token.mint`).\n\n---\n\n## 7. Extensibility Guidelines\n\n1. Implement the respective interface (`IConsensusEngine`, `IStakingCalculator`,\n   etc.).\n2. Register your type in `module.json`:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"consensus\": \"MyCompany.UtilityChain.PowEngine, MyCompany.UtilityChain\"\n}\n```\n\n3. Rebuild the monolith:  \n   `dotnet publish -c Release -r linux-x64 --single-file`\n\n---\n\n## 8. Build & Deployment\n\n```bash\n# Build\ndotnet restore\ndotnet build -c Release\n\n# Publish self-contained executable\ndotnet publish -c Release -r win-x64 --self-contained true /p:PublishSingleFile=true\n```\n\nThe resulting binary (`utilitychain.exe` or `utilitychain`) is the only file\nrequired on the target host.\n\n---\n\n## 9. Observability\n\n- **Structured logging** via `Serilog` to Console, File, Seq.\n- **Metrics** exposed on `/metrics` (Prometheus).\n- **Health Checks** (`/health/live`, `/health/ready`).\n\n---\n\n## 10. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01DP67yfeJTJtkxBwnuVMaXs","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→```markdown\n     2→# ADR-003: Event-Driven Module Communication\n     3→UtilityChain Core Suite  \n     4→Created: 2024-05-26  \n     5→Status: **Accepted**\n     6→\n     7→---\n     8→\n     9→## 1. Context\n    10→UtilityChain is delivered as a single, self-contained .NET 8 executable.  \n    11→Although a monolith, it is _modular_: **Staking**, **Consensus**, **Governance**, **Token/NFT Tooling**, and the **Smart-Contract Engine** are developed as pluggable components that must interoperate without compile-time coupling.\n    12→\n    13→Key forces:\n    14→\n    15→| Force | Explanation |\n    16→|-------|-------------|\n    17→| Runtime Efficiency | Blockchain nodes must achieve deterministic, micro-second latency. |\n    18→| Hot-swappability  | New consensus engines, staking algorithms, or NFT standards may be introduced without shutting down a node. |\n    19→| Isolation         | A failure in one module (e.g., a buggy smart-contract) must not crash the entire node. |\n    20→| Observability     | Administrators need fine-grained telemetry (audit, tracing, metrics) for each module. |\n    21→\n    22→Traditional approaches (direct method calls, REST gRPC, message brokers) either introduce undesirable coupling or unacceptable latency.  \n    23→Therefore, an **in-process event bus** (mediator) will be the spine of module communication.\n    24→\n    25→---\n    26→\n    27→## 2. Decision\n    28→1. Adopt a lightweight, in-memory **Domain Event Bus** that follows the _Mediator_ and _Observer_ patterns.  \n    29→2. Events are C# records implementing the `IApplicationEvent` marker interface to ensure immutability and serialization safety.  \n    30→3. Modules interact exclusively through events and **Application Commands**; no module holds a hard reference to another.  \n    31→4. Subscriptions are registered at runtime through a **Fluent Subscription API** allowing hot-plug and graceful unloading.  \n    32→5. The bus supports:\n    33→   • Synchronous “fire-and-forget” events  \n    34→   • Asynchronous events with `Task` pipelines  \n    35→   • Cancellation tokens for long-running work  \n    36→   • Weak references to prevent memory leaks  \n    37→6. Observability is baked in via the `IEventTracer` abstraction; a default OpenTelemetry implementation ships with the core suite.  \n    38→\n    39→---\n    40→\n    41→## 3. Consequences\n    42→✔  Runtime coupling is reduced to the event contract; modules evolve independently.  \n    43→✔  Zero-copy, in-process dispatch keeps latency in the sub-millisecond range.  \n    44→✔  New modules may be loaded via reflection or MEF without recompilation.  \n    45→✖  Debugging becomes harder because control-flow is no longer explicit in the call-stack.  \n    46→✖  Misuse of long-running handlers can still block the thread if not awaited or scheduled correctly.  \n    47→\n    48→---\n    49→\n    50→## 4. Implementation Sketch (Production-Quality C#)\n    51→\n    52→```csharp\n    53→using System.Collections.Concurrent;\n    54→using System.Diagnostics;\n    55→using System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis;\n    56→using System.Runtime.CompilerServices;\n    57→\n    58→namespace UtilityChain.Core.Eventing;\n    59→\n    60→/// <summary>\n    61→/// Marker interface for immutable domain events that flow through the in-process event bus.\n    62→/// </summary>\n    63→public interface IApplicationEvent { }\n    64→\n    65→/// <summary>\n    66→/// A canonical contract for consuming <typeparamref name=\"TEvent\" /> messages.\n    67→/// Handlers should be stateless and idempotent.  \n    68→/// </summary>\n    69→public interface IEventHandler<in TEvent> where TEvent : IApplicationEvent\n    70→{\n    71→    /// <summary>Handle an application event.</summary>\n    72→    ValueTask HandleAsync(TEvent @event, CancellationToken ct);\n    73→}\n    74→\n    75→/// <summary>\n    76→/// A handle that allows the subscriber to dispose/unsubscribe at runtime.\n    77→/// </summary>\n    78→public sealed class Subscription : IDisposable\n    79→{\n    80→    private readonly Action _dispose;\n    81→    private int _disposed;\n    82→\n    83→    internal Subscription(Action dispose) => _dispose = dispose;\n    84→\n    85→    public void Dispose()\n    86→    {\n    87→        if (Interlocked.Exchange(ref _disposed, 1) == 0)\n    88→            _dispose();\n    89→    }\n    90→}\n    91→\n    92→/// <summary>\n    93→/// Core implementation of the event bus.  It is intentionally lightweight, allocation-free for the hot-path,\n    94→/// and free of third-party dependencies so that it can operate in AOT contexts.\n    95→/// </summary>\n    96→public sealed class EventBus : IEventTracer\n    97→{\n    98→    // Handlers are stored in a thread-safe dictionary keyed by event type.\n    99→    private readonly ConcurrentDictionary<Type, ConcurrentBag<WeakReference<object>>> _handlers = new();\n   100→\n   101→    // External tracer for observability; can be swapped for OpenTelemetry/Jaeger/etc.\n   102→    private readonly IEventTracer _tracer;\n   103→\n   104→    public EventBus(IEventTracer? tracer = null)\n   105→    {\n   106→        _tracer = tracer ?? this; // Fallback to self-logging.\n   107→    }\n   108→\n   109→    #region Publish\n   110→\n   111→    /// <summary>\n   112→    /// Publish an event in a fire-and-forget manner.\n   113→    /// </summary>\n   114→    public void Publish<TEvent>(TEvent @event) where TEvent : IApplicationEvent\n   115→        => PublishAsync(@event, CancellationToken.None).GetAwaiter().GetResult();\n   116→\n   117→    /// <summary>\n   118→    /// Publish an event asynchronously.  Handlers are awaited _concurrently_.\n   119→    /// </summary>\n   120→    public async ValueTask PublishAsync<TEvent>(\n   121→        TEvent @event,\n   122→        CancellationToken ct = default) where TEvent : IApplicationEvent\n   123→    {\n   124→        if (_handlers.TryGetValue(typeof(TEvent), out var list))\n   125→        {\n   126→            var tasks = new List<ValueTask>(list.Count);\n   127→            foreach (var weak in list)\n   128→            {\n   129→                if (weak.TryGetTarget(out var target) &&\n   130→                    target is IEventHandler<TEvent> handler)\n   131→                {\n   132→                    tasks.Add(InvokeHandler(handler, @event, ct));\n   133→                }\n   134→            }\n   135→            await ValueTask.WhenAll(tasks);\n   136→        }\n   137→        else\n   138→        {\n   139→            _tracer.TraceNoHandler(@event);\n   140→        }\n   141→    }\n   142→\n   143→    [MethodImpl(MethodImplOptions.AggressiveInlining)]\n   144→    private async ValueTask InvokeHandler<TEvent>(\n   145→        IEventHandler<TEvent> handler,\n   146→        TEvent @event,\n   147→        CancellationToken ct) where TEvent : IApplicationEvent\n   148→    {\n   149→        var sw = Stopwatch.StartNew();\n   150→        try\n   151→        {\n   152→            await handler.HandleAsync(@event, ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n   153→            _tracer.TraceSuccess(@event, handler, sw.Elapsed);\n   154→        }\n   155→        catch (OperationCanceledException) when (ct.IsCancellationRequested)\n   156→        {\n   157→            _tracer.TraceCancelled(@event, handler, sw.Elapsed);\n   158→            throw;\n   159→        }\n   160→        catch (Exception ex)\n   161→        {\n   162→            _tracer.TraceFailure(@event, handler, sw.Elapsed, ex);\n   163→            throw;\n   164→        }\n   165→    }\n   166→\n   167→    #endregion\n   168→\n   169→    #region Subscribe\n   170→\n   171→    /// <summary>\n   172→    /// Subscribe an <see cref=\"IEventHandler{TEvent}\" /> instance to the bus.\n   173→    /// Returns a <see cref=\"Subscription\" /> that can be disposed to unsubscribe.\n   174→    /// </summary>\n   175→    public Subscription Subscribe<TEvent>(IEventHandler<TEvent> handler)\n   176→        where TEvent : IApplicationEvent\n   177→    {\n   178→        var bag = _handlers.GetOrAdd(typeof(TEvent), _ => new ConcurrentBag<WeakReference<object>>());\n   179→        var weak = new WeakReference<object>(handler);\n   180→        bag.Add(weak);\n   181→\n   182→        return new Subscription(() => RemoveHandler(typeof(TEvent), weak));\n   183→    }\n   184→\n   185→    private void RemoveHandler(Type type, WeakReference<object> weak)\n   186→    {\n   187→        if (_handlers.TryGetValue(type, out var list))\n   188→        {\n   189→            // ConcurrentBag does not support removal; rebuild without the disposed handler.\n   190→            var newBag = new ConcurrentBag<WeakReference<object>>(list.Where(wr => wr != weak));\n   191→            _handlers[type] = newBag;\n   192→        }\n   193→    }\n   194→\n   195→    #endregion\n   196→\n   197→    #region Self-Trace (No-op)\n   198→\n   199→    void IEventTracer.TraceNoHandler(IApplicationEvent ev) { }\n   200→    void IEventTracer.TraceSuccess(IApplicationEvent ev, object handler, TimeSpan dur) { }\n   201→    void IEventTracer.TraceCancelled(IApplicationEvent ev, object handler, TimeSpan dur) { }\n   202→    void IEventTracer.TraceFailure(IApplicationEvent ev, object handler, TimeSpan dur, Exception ex) { }\n   203→\n   204→    #endregion\n   205→}\n   206→\n   207→/// <summary>\n   208→/// Defines tracing callbacks for diagnostics.  Implementations should be side-effect-free.\n   209→/// </summary>\n   210→public interface IEventTracer\n   211→{\n   212→    void TraceNoHandler(IApplicationEvent ev);\n   213→    void TraceSuccess(IApplicationEvent ev, object handler, TimeSpan dur);\n   214→    void TraceCancelled(IApplicationEvent ev, object handler, TimeSpan dur);\n   215→    void TraceFailure(IApplicationEvent ev, object handler, TimeSpan dur, Exception ex);\n   216→}\n   217→```\n   218→\n   219→### Usage Example\n   220→\n   221→```csharp\n   222→// 1. Create a concrete event\n   223→public sealed record TokensMinted(Guid TokenId, decimal Amount, Address Owner) : IApplicationEvent;\n   224→\n   225→// 2. Implement a handler that reacts to the event\n   226→public sealed class LedgerProjection : IEventHandler<TokensMinted>\n   227→{\n   228→    private readonly ILedgerRepository _repo;\n   229→    public LedgerProjection(ILedgerRepository repo) => _repo = repo;\n   230→\n   231→    public async ValueTask HandleAsync(TokensMinted e, CancellationToken ct)\n   232→    {\n   233→        var entry = new LedgerEntry(e.TokenId, e.Owner, e.Amount, LedgerOp.Mint);\n   234→        await _repo.AddAsync(entry, ct);\n   235→    }\n   236→}\n   237→\n   238→// 3. Wire up in the composition root\n   239→var bus = new EventBus(new OpenTelemetryTracer());\n   240→var projection = new LedgerProjection(repo);\n   241→using var sub = bus.Subscribe(projection);\n   242→\n   243→// 4. Somewhere in the Token module…\n   244→await bus.PublishAsync(new TokensMinted(tokenId, 250, caller.Address), ct);\n   245→```\n   246→\n   247→---\n   248→\n   249→## 5. Future Work\n   250→• Integrate a deterministic, priority-aware scheduler so that consensus-critical events pre-empt UI telemetry.  \n   251→• Provide Roslyn source generators for boiler-plate handler registration.  \n   252→• Investigate out-of-process fallbacks (e.g., NATS, Kafka) for horizontally scaled, multi-process deployments.\n   253→\n   254→---\n   255→© 2024 UtilityChain Contributors – Released under the MIT License\n   256→```\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. Context\nUtilityChain is delivered as a single, self-contained .NET 8 executable.  \nAlthough a monolith, it is _modular_: **Staking**, **Consensus**, **Governance**, **Token/NFT Tooling**, and the **Smart-Contract Engine** are developed as pluggable components that must interoperate without compile-time coupling.\n\nKey forces:\n\n| Force | Explanation |\n|-------|-------------|\n| Runtime Efficiency | Blockchain nodes must achieve deterministic, micro-second latency. |\n| Hot-swappability  | New consensus engines, staking algorithms, or NFT standards may be introduced without shutting down a node. |\n| Isolation         | A failure in one module (e.g., a buggy smart-contract) must not crash the entire node. |\n| Observability     | Administrators need fine-grained telemetry (audit, tracing, metrics) for each module. |\n\nTraditional approaches (direct method calls, REST gRPC, message brokers) either introduce undesirable coupling or unacceptable latency.  \nTherefore, an **in-process event bus** (mediator) will be the spine of module communication.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Decision\n1. Adopt a lightweight, in-memory **Domain Event Bus** that follows the _Mediator_ and _Observer_ patterns.  \n2. Events are C# records implementing the `IApplicationEvent` marker interface to ensure immutability and serialization safety.  \n3. Modules interact exclusively through events and **Application Commands**; no module holds a hard reference to another.  \n4. Subscriptions are registered at runtime through a **Fluent Subscription API** allowing hot-plug and graceful unloading.  \n5. The bus supports:\n   • Synchronous “fire-and-forget” events  \n   • Asynchronous events with `Task` pipelines  \n   • Cancellation tokens for long-running work  \n   • Weak references to prevent memory leaks  \n6. Observability is baked in via the `IEventTracer` abstraction; a default OpenTelemetry implementation ships with the core suite.  \n\n---\n\n## 3. Consequences\n✔  Runtime coupling is reduced to the event contract; modules evolve independently.  \n✔  Zero-copy, in-process dispatch keeps latency in the sub-millisecond range.  \n✔  New modules may be loaded via reflection or MEF without recompilation.  \n✖  Debugging becomes harder because control-flow is no longer explicit in the call-stack.  \n✖  Misuse of long-running handlers can still block the thread if not awaited or scheduled correctly.  \n\n---\n\n## 4. Implementation Sketch (Production-Quality C#)\n\n```csharp\nusing System.Collections.Concurrent;\nusing System.Diagnostics;\nusing System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis;\nusing System.Runtime.CompilerServices;\n\nnamespace UtilityChain.Core.Eventing;\n\n/// <summary>\n/// Marker interface for immutable domain events that flow through the in-process event bus.\n/// </summary>\npublic interface IApplicationEvent { }\n\n/// <summary>\n/// A canonical contract for consuming <typeparamref name=\"TEvent\" /> messages.\n/// Handlers should be stateless and idempotent.  \n/// </summary>\npublic interface IEventHandler<in TEvent> where TEvent : IApplicationEvent\n{\n    /// <summary>Handle an application event.</summary>\n    ValueTask HandleAsync(TEvent @event, CancellationToken ct);\n}\n\n/// <summary>\n/// A handle that allows the subscriber to dispose/unsubscribe at runtime.\n/// </summary>\npublic sealed class Subscription : IDisposable\n{\n    private readonly Action _dispose;\n    private int _disposed;\n\n    internal Subscription(Action dispose) => _dispose = dispose;\n\n    public void Dispose()\n    {\n        if (Interlocked.Exchange(ref _disposed, 1) == 0)\n            _dispose();\n    }\n}\n\n/// <summary>\n/// Core implementation of the event bus.  It is intentionally lightweight, allocation-free for the hot-path,\n/// and free of third-party dependencies so that it can operate in AOT contexts.\n/// </summary>\npublic sealed class EventBus : IEventTracer\n{\n    // Handlers are stored in a thread-safe dictionary keyed by event type.\n    private readonly ConcurrentDictionary<Type, ConcurrentBag<WeakReference<object>>> _handlers = new();\n\n    // External tracer for observability; can be swapped for OpenTelemetry/Jaeger/etc.\n    private readonly IEventTracer _tracer;\n\n    public EventBus(IEventTracer? tracer = null)\n    {\n        _tracer = tracer ?? this; // Fallback to self-logging.\n    }\n\n    #region Publish\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Publish an event in a fire-and-forget manner.\n    /// </summary>\n    public void Publish<TEvent>(TEvent @event) where TEvent : IApplicationEvent\n        => PublishAsync(@event, CancellationToken.None).GetAwaiter().GetResult();\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Publish an event asynchronously.  Handlers are awaited _concurrently_.\n    /// </summary>\n    public async ValueTask PublishAsync<TEvent>(\n        TEvent @event,\n        CancellationToken ct = default) where TEvent : IApplicationEvent\n    {\n        if (_handlers.TryGetValue(typeof(TEvent), out var list))\n        {\n            var tasks = new List<ValueTask>(list.Count);\n            foreach (var weak in list)\n            {\n                if (weak.TryGetTarget(out var target) &&\n                    target is IEventHandler<TEvent> handler)\n                {\n                    tasks.Add(InvokeHandler(handler, @event, ct));\n                }\n            }\n            await ValueTask.WhenAll(tasks);\n        }\n        else\n        {\n            _tracer.TraceNoHandler(@event);\n        }\n    }\n\n    [MethodImpl(MethodImplOptions.AggressiveInlining)]\n    private async ValueTask InvokeHandler<TEvent>(\n        IEventHandler<TEvent> handler,\n        TEvent @event,\n        CancellationToken ct) where TEvent : IApplicationEvent\n    {\n        var sw = Stopwatch.StartNew();\n        try\n        {\n            await handler.HandleAsync(@event, ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n            _tracer.TraceSuccess(@event, handler, sw.Elapsed);\n        }\n        catch (OperationCanceledException) when (ct.IsCancellationRequested)\n        {\n            _tracer.TraceCancelled(@event, handler, sw.Elapsed);\n            throw;\n        }\n        catch (Exception ex)\n        {\n            _tracer.TraceFailure(@event, handler, sw.Elapsed, ex);\n            throw;\n        }\n    }\n\n    #endregion\n\n    #region Subscribe\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Subscribe an <see cref=\"IEventHandler{TEvent}\" /> instance to the bus.\n    /// Returns a <see cref=\"Subscription\" /> that can be disposed to unsubscribe.\n    /// </summary>\n    public Subscription Subscribe<TEvent>(IEventHandler<TEvent> handler)\n        where TEvent : IApplicationEvent\n    {\n        var bag = _handlers.GetOrAdd(typeof(TEvent), _ => new ConcurrentBag<WeakReference<object>>());\n        var weak = new WeakReference<object>(handler);\n        bag.Add(weak);\n\n        return new Subscription(() => RemoveHandler(typeof(TEvent), weak));\n    }\n\n    private void RemoveHandler(Type type, WeakReference<object> weak)\n    {\n        if (_handlers.TryGetValue(type, out var list))\n        {\n            // ConcurrentBag does not support removal; rebuild without the disposed handler.\n            var newBag = new ConcurrentBag<WeakReference<object>>(list.Where(wr => wr != weak));\n            _handlers[type] = newBag;\n        }\n    }\n\n    #endregion\n\n    #region Self-Trace (No-op)\n\n    void IEventTracer.TraceNoHandler(IApplicationEvent ev) { }\n    void IEventTracer.TraceSuccess(IApplicationEvent ev, object handler, TimeSpan dur) { }\n    void IEventTracer.TraceCancelled(IApplicationEvent ev, object handler, TimeSpan dur) { }\n    void IEventTracer.TraceFailure(IApplicationEvent ev, object handler, TimeSpan dur, Exception ex) { }\n\n    #endregion\n}\n\n/// <summary>\n/// Defines tracing callbacks for diagnostics.  Implementations should be side-effect-free.\n/// </summary>\npublic interface IEventTracer\n{\n    void TraceNoHandler(IApplicationEvent ev);\n    void TraceSuccess(IApplicationEvent ev, object handler, TimeSpan dur);\n    void TraceCancelled(IApplicationEvent ev, object handler, TimeSpan dur);\n    void TraceFailure(IApplicationEvent ev, object handler, TimeSpan dur, Exception ex);\n}\n```\n\n### Usage Example\n\n```csharp\n// 1. Create a concrete event\npublic sealed record TokensMinted(Guid TokenId, decimal Amount, Address Owner) : IApplicationEvent;\n\n// 2. Implement a handler that reacts to the event\npublic sealed class LedgerProjection : IEventHandler<TokensMinted>\n{\n    private readonly ILedgerRepository _repo;\n    public LedgerProjection(ILedgerRepository repo) => _repo = repo;\n\n    public async ValueTask HandleAsync(TokensMinted e, CancellationToken ct)\n    {\n        var entry = new LedgerEntry(e.TokenId, e.Owner, e.Amount, LedgerOp.Mint);\n        await _repo.AddAsync(entry, ct);\n    }\n}\n\n// 3. Wire up in the composition root\nvar bus = new EventBus(new OpenTelemetryTracer());\nvar projection = new LedgerProjection(repo);\nusing var sub = bus.Subscribe(projection);\n\n// 4. Somewhere in the Token module…\nawait bus.PublishAsync(new TokensMinted(tokenId, 250, caller.Address), ct);\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Future Work\n• Integrate a deterministic, priority-aware scheduler so that consensus-critical events pre-empt UI telemetry.  \n• Provide Roslyn source generators for boiler-plate handler registration.  \n• Investigate out-of-process fallbacks (e.g., NATS, Kafka) for horizontally scaled, multi-process deployments.\n\n---\n© 2024 UtilityChain Contributors – Released under the MIT License\n```","numLines":256,"startLine":1,"totalLines":256}}}

do.\n            return;\n        }\n\n        foreach (var sub in subscribers.Values)\n        {\n            // Fire-and-forget: each handler is executed independently.\n            _ = Task.Run(async () =>\n            {\n                var sw = ValueStopwatch.StartNew();\n                try\n                {\n                    await sub.Handler(envelope.Event, {\n                    _logger.LogError(ex,\n                        \"Error delivering event '{EventType}' to subscriber '{SubscriberId}'\",\n                        envelope.Event.GetType().Name, sub.Id);\n                }\n            });\n        }\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Helpers\n    // System.Threading.Channels;\nusing System.Threading.Tasks;\nusing Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;\n\nnamespace UtilityChain.TransactionProcessing;\n\n/// <summary>\n/// Coordinates the full transaction-lifecycle pipeline: validation, persistence, broadcasting,\n/// consensus pre-checks, and state-change notifications.  \n/// The processor is intentionally stateless except for a bounded in-memory\n/// inflight queue to guarantee back-pressure and prevent mem-exhaustion DoS vectors.\n/// </summary>\npublic sealed class TransactionProcessor : ITransactionProcessor, IAsyncDisposable\n{\n    private const int DefaultQueueCapacity = 4_096;\n\n    private readonly Channel<TransactionEnvelope> _queue;\n    private readonly ILogger<TransactionProcessor> _logger;\n <summary>\n    /// Initializes a new <see cref=\"TransactionProcessor\"/> instance.\n    /// All dependencies are expected to be wired through DI.\n    /// </summary>\n    public TransactionProcessor(\n        ILogger<TransactionProcessor> logger,\n        IEnumerable<ITransactionValidator> validators,\n        ITransactionRepository repository,\n        IConsensusOracle consensusOracle,\n        IPeerBroadcaster broadcaster,\n        IEventBus eventBus,\n        TransactionProcessorOptions? options = null)\n    {\n        _logger = logger ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(logger));\n        _validators = validators?.ToArray() ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(validators));\n        _repository = repository ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(repository));\n        _consensusOracle = consensusOracle ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(consensusOracle));\n        _broadcaster = broadcaster ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(broadcaster));\n        _eventBus = eventBus ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(eventBus));\n\n        options ??= TransactionProcessorOptions.Default;\n        _queue = Channel.CreateBounded<TransactionEnvelope>(\n            new BoundedChannelOptions(options.QueueCapacity ?? DefaultQueueCapacity)\n            {\n                AllowSynchronousContinuations = false,\n                FullMode = BoundedChannelFullMode.Wait\n            });\n\n        _worker = Task.Factory.StartNew(ProcessLoopAsync,\n            _cts.Token,\n            TaskCreationOptions.LongRunning,\n            TaskScheduler.Default).Unwrap();\n    }\n\n    #region ITransactionProcessor\n\n    /// <inheritdoc/>\n    public async ValueTask SubmitAsync(Transaction transaction, CancellationToken ct = default)\n    {\n        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(transaction);\n\n        if (!await _queue.Writer.WaitToWriteAsync(ct).ConfigureAwait(false))\n        {\n            throw new InvalidOperationException(\"Transaction queue is closed.\");\n        }\n\n        // Avoid leaking user token into background pipeline\n        var envelope = new TransactionEnvelope(transaction, DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);\n        await _queue.Writer.WriteAsync(envelope, ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n    }\n\n    #endregion\n\n    #region Background Processing\n\n    private async Task ProcessLoopAsync()\n    {\n        _logger.LogInformation(\"TransactionProcessor event loop started.\");\n\n        await foreach (var envelope in _queue.Reader.ReadAllAsync(_cts.Token))\n        {\n            try\n            {\n                await HandleTransactionAsync(envelope, _cts.Token).ConfigureAwait(false);\n            }\n            catch (OperationCanceledException) when (_cts.IsCancellationRequested)\n            {\n                // expected on shutdown\n            }\n            catch (Exception ex)\n            {\n                _logger.LogError(ex, \"Fatal error while handling transaction {TxHash}.  Transaction dropped.\",\n                    envelope.Transaction.Hash);\n            }\n        }\n\n        _logger.LogInformation(\"TransactionProcessor event loop stopped.\");\n    }\n\n    private async Task HandleTransactionAsync(TransactionEnvelope envelope, CancellationToken ct)\n    {\n        var tx = envelope.Transaction;\n        _logger.LogDebug(\"Handling transaction {TxHash}.\", tx.Hash);\n\n        // 1. Run validation pipeline\n        foreach (var validator in _validators)\n        {\n            var result = await validator.ValidateAsync(tx, ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n            if (!result.IsValid)\n            {\n                _logger.LogWarning(\"Transaction {TxHash} rejected by validator {Validator} – {Reason}.\",\n                    tx.Hash, validator.GetType().Name, result.Diagnostic);\n                await _eventBus.PublishAsync(new TransactionRejectedEvent(tx, result.Diagnostic), ct);\n                return;\n            }\n        }\n\n        // 2. Consensus pre-admission check (e.g., mempool sizing, fee sufficiency)\n        if (!_consensusOracle.MayAccept(tx))\n        {\n            _logger.LogWarning(\"Transaction {TxHash} did not satisfy consensus pre-check. Rejected.\", tx.Hash);\n            await _eventBus.PublishAsync(new TransactionRejectedEvent(tx, \"Consensus conditions not met.\"), ct);\n            return;\n        }\n\n        // 3. Persist into repository (mempool/pending state)\n        try\n        {\n            await _repository.AddPendingAsync(tx, ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n        }\n        catch (DuplicateTransactionException)\n        {\n            _logger.LogInformation(\"Duplicate transaction {TxHash} ignored.\", tx.Hash);\n            return;\n        }\n\n        // 4. Broadcast to peers (fire-and-forget, but log failure)\n        _ = Task.Run(async () =>\n        {\n            try\n            {\n                await _broadcaster.BroadcastAsync(tx, _cts.Token).ConfigureAwait(false);\n            }\n            catch (Exception ex)\n            {\n                _logger.LogWarning(ex, \"Failed to broadcast transaction {TxHash}.\", tx.Hash);\n            }\n        }, CancellationToken.None);\n\n        // 5. Notify local subscribers\n        await _eventBus.PublishAsync(new TransactionAcceptedEvent(tx), ct);\n        _logger.LogInformation(\"Transaction {TxHash} accepted.\", tx.Hash);\n    }\n\n    #endregion\n\n    #region IAsyncDisposable\n\n    /// <inheritdoc/>\n    public async ValueTask DisposeAsync()\n    {\n        try\n        {\n            _cts.Cancel();\n\n            _queue.Writer.TryComplete();\n\n            await _worker.ConfigureAwait(false);\n        }\n        finally\n        {\n            _cts.Dispose();\n        }\n    }\n\n    #endregion\n\n    #region Nested Types / Options\n\n    private sealed record TransactionEnvelope(Transaction Transaction, DateTimeOffset ReceivedAtUtc);\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Configurable behaviour for the <see cref=\"TransactionProcessor\"/>.\n    /// Provided via DI options pattern.\n    /// </summary>\n    public sealed record TransactionProcessorOptions\n    {\n        public static readonly TransactionProcessorOptions Default = new();\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Maximum number of inflight items kept in memory before back-pressure triggers.\n        /// </summary>\n        public int? QueueCapacity { get; init; } = DefaultQueueCapacity;\n    }\n\n    #endregion\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n *  Below are domain contracts referenced by TransactionProcessor; they are intentionally\n *  minimal to avoid leaking irrelevant implementation details while keeping this file\n *  self-contained and compilable in isolation of the rest of the monolith.\n * --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n#region Domain Contracts (abbreviated)\n\npublic interface ITransactionProcessor\n{\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Queues a transaction for processing. New total: {Total}.\",\n                address, amount, lockPeriod, account.TotalStaked);\n\n            _eventBus.Publish(new StakeCreatedEvent(stake, account.TotalStaked));\n\n            return new StakeReceipt(stake.StakeId, stake.CreatedAt);\n        }\n        finally\n        {\n            account.Semaphore.Release();\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// <inheritdoc />\n    public async Task<UnstakeReceipt> UnstakeAsync(\n        Address address, StakeId stakeId, CancellationToken ct = default)\n    {\n        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(address);\n        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(stakeId);\n\n        if (!_accounts.TryGetValue(address, out var account))\n            throw new StakeNotFoundException(stakeId);\n\n        await account.Semaphore.WaitAsync(ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n        try\n        {\n            var stake = account.GetStake(stakeId);\n\n            if (stake == null)\n                throw new StakeNotFoundException(stakeId);\n\n            if (stake.State != StakeState.Locked &&\n                stake.State != StakeState.Released)\n                throw new InvalidStakeTransitionException(stake.State, StakeState.Withdrawn);\n\n            if (stake.State == StakeState.Locked &&\n                _clock.Now < stake.UnlocksAt)\n                throw new StakeLockedException(stake.UnlocksAt);\n\n            stake.State = StakeState.Withdrawn;\n            account.TotalStaked -= stake.Amount;\n\n            _logger.LogInformation(\n                \"Stake {StakeId} withdrawn. Address {Address} remaining total: {Total}.\",\n                stakeId, address, account.TotalStaked);\n\n            _eventBus.Publish(new StakeWithdrawnEvent(stake));\n\n            return new UnstakeReceipt(stake.StakeId, _clock.Now, stake.Amount);\n        }\n        finally\n        {\n            account.Semaphore.Release();\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// <inheritdoc />\n    public async Task<IReadOnlyCollection<StakeInfo>> GetStakesAsync(Address address,\n        CancellationToken ct = default)\n    {\n        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(address);\n\n        if (!_accounts.TryGetValue(address, out var account))\n            return Array.Empty<StakeInfo>();\n\n        await account.Semaphore.WaitAsync(ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n        try\n        {\n            return account.ListStakes();\n        }\n        finally\n        {\n            account.Semaphore.Release();\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// <inheritdoc />\n    public async Task<decimal> GetAccumulatedRewardsAsync(Address address,\n        CancellationToken ct = default)\n    {\n        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(address);\n\n        if (!_accounts.TryGetValue(address, out var account))\n            return 0m;\n\n        await account.Semaphore.WaitAsync(ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n        try\n        {\n            var rewards = _rewardCalculator.Calculate(account, _clock.Now);\n\n            return rewards;\n        }\n        finally\n        {\n            account.Semaphore.Release();\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// <inheritdoc />\n    public async Task ClaimRewardsAsync(Address address, CancellationToken ct = default)\n    {\n        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(address);\n\n        if (!_accounts.TryGetValue(address, out var account))\n            throw new StakingException(\"Account not found.\");\n\n        await account.Semaphore.WaitAsync(ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n        try\n        {\n            var rewards = _rewardCalculator.Calculate(account, _clock.Now);\n\n            if (rewards <= 0m)\n                throw new StakingException(\"No rewards available.\");\n\n            account.RewardsClaimed += rewards;\n\n            _logger.LogInformation(\"Address {Address} claimed {Rewards} rewards\", address, rewards);\n\n            _eventBus.Publish(new RewardsClaimedEvent(address, rewards));\n        }\n        finally\n        {\n            account.Semaphore.Release();\n        }\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Event handling\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Called for every new block to update lock expirations and reward indexes\n    private void OnNewBlock(NewBlockEvent e)\n    {\n        foreach (var (_, account) in _accounts)\n        {\n            // Intentionally fire-and-forget; minimal per-block CPU time\n            _ = Task.Run(() =>\n            {\n                if (!account.Semaphore.Wait(0))\n                    return; // Skip busy accounts to maintain block throughput\n\n                try\n                {\n                    account.ProcessBlock(_clock.Now);\n                }\n                catch (Exception ex)\n                {\n                    _logger.LogError(ex, \"Failed to process staking updates for address {Address}.\",\n                        account.Address);\n                }\n                finally\n                {\n                    account.Semaphore.Release();\n                }\n            }, _cts.Token);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Background persistence loop\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    private async Task BackgroundSyncLoopAsync()\n    {\n        var token = _cts.Token;\n\n        try\n        {\n            while (!token.IsCancellationRequested)\n            {\n                await Task.Delay(TimeSpan.FromMinutes(1), token).ConfigureAwait(false);\n\n                foreach (var (_, account) in _accounts)\n                {\n                    if (!account.Semaphore.Wait(0))\n                        continue; // Skip active accounts\n\n                    try\n                    {\n                        var snapshot = account.CreateSnapshot();\n                        await _repository.SaveAsync(snapshot, token).ConfigureAwait(false);\n                    }\n                    catch (Exception ex)\n                    {\n                        _logger.LogWarning(ex, \"Failed to persist staking snapshot for {Address}.\",\n                            account.Address);\n                    }\n                    finally\n                    {\n                        account.Semaphore.Release();\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        catch (OperationCanceledException)\n        {\n            // Normal shutdown\n        }\n        catch (Exception ex)\n        {\n            _logger.LogCritical(ex, \"Unexpected failure in staking background loop.\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Disposable\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    public async ValueTask DisposeAsync()\n    {\n        _cts.Cancel();\n\n        _eventBus.Unsubscribe<NewBlockEvent>(OnNewBlock);\n\n        try\n        {\n            await _syncTask.ConfigureAwait(false);\n        }\n        catch (Exception ex)\n        {\n            _logger.LogWarning(ex, \"Failure while stopping staking service sync loop.\");\n        }\n\n        _cts.Dispose();\n    }\n\n    // #region Domain\n\n    private sealed class StakeAccount\n    {\n        private readonly ConcurrentDictionary<StakeId, Stake> _stakes = new();\n        internal readonly SemaphoreSlim Semaphore             = new(1, 1);\n\n        internal StakeAccount(Address address)\n        {\n            Address = address;\n            Created = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow;\n        }\n\n        internal Address    {\n                stake.TryRelease(now);\n            }\n        }\n\n        internal IReadOnlyCollection<StakeInfo> ListStakes()\n        {\n            var list = new }\n\n        internal StakeAccountSnapshot CreateSnapshot()\n        {\n            var stakeSnapshots = new List<StakeSnapshot>();\n\n            foreach (var s in _stakes.Values)\n            {\n                stakeSnapshots.Add(new StakeSnapshot(\n                    s.StakeId,\n                    s.Amount,\n                    s.CreatedAt,\n                    s.UnlocksAt,\n                    s.State));\n            }\n\n            return new StakeAccountSnapshot(\n                Address,\n                Created,\n                TotalStaked,\n                RewardsClaimed,\n                stakeSnapshots);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private sealed class Stake\n    {\n        private static readonly RNGCryptoServiceProvider Rng = new();\n\n        internal Stake(ulong amount, StakeState.Locked;\n            StakeId   = GenerateId();\n        }\n\n        internal ulong           Amount     { get; }\n        internal DateTimeOffset  CreatedAt  { get; }\n        internal DateTimeOffset  UnlocksAt  { get; }\n        internal StakeState      State      { get; set; }\n        internal StakeId         StakeId    { get; }\n\n        internal void TryRelease(DateTimeOffset public decimal Calculate(object accountObj, DateTimeOffset now)\n        {\n            if (accountObj is not StakingService.StakeAccount account)\n                throw new         {\n                // Attempt to finalize asynchronously—do not block caller\n                _ = Task.Run(() => FinalizeAsync(aggregate, default), ct);\n                throw new GovernanceException(\"Voting period has already elapsed.\");\n            }\n\n            var vote = new Vote(\n                aggregate.Proposal.Id,\n                voterAddress,\n                voteType,\n                _clock.UtcNow);\n\n            if (aggregate.HasVoted(voterAddress))\n                throw new GovernanceException(\"Voter has already cast a vote on this proposal.\");\n\n            if (!_policies.All(p => p.ValidateVote(aggregate.Proposal, vote)))\n                throw new GovernanceException(\"One or more policies rejected the vote.\");\n\n            _lifecycleLock.EnterWriteLock();\n            try\n            {\n                aggregate.AddVote(vote);\n            }\n            finally\n            {\n                _lifecycleLock.ExitWriteLock();\n            }\n\n            _eventBus.Publish(new VoteCastEvent(vote));\n\n            return new VoteReceipt(vote, aggregate.Tally());\n        }\n        finally\n        {\n            _lifecycleLock.ExitUpgradeableReadLock();\n        }\n    }\n\n    public ProposalTally GetCurrentTally(Guid proposalId)\n    {\n        ThrowIfDisposed();\n\n        if .Where(p => status is null || p.Status == status)\n                         .OrderByDescending(p => p.CreatedAt)\n                         .ToList(); // Materialize enumeration for thread-safety\n _lifecycleLock.EnterWriteLock();\n        try\n        {\n            if (aggregate.Proposal.Status is ProposalStatus.Finalized)\n                return; // Idempotent\n\n            var tally = aggregate.Tally();\n\n            var decision = _policies\n                .Select(p => p.EvaluateResult(aggregate.Proposal, tally))\n                .Aggregate(ProposalDecision.Undecided, CombineDecisions);\n\n            aggregate.Finalize(_clock.UtcNow, decision);\n\n            _eventBus.Publish(new ProposalFinalizedEvent(aggregate.Proposal, tally));\n        }\n        finally\n        {\n            _lifecycleLock.ExitWriteLock();\n        }\n\n        await Task.CompletedTask;\n    }\n\n    private static ProposalDecision CombineDecisions(ProposalDecision first, ProposalDecision second)\n    {\n        // If any policy rejects, the proposal is rejected.\n        if (first == ProposalDecision.Rejected || second == ProposalDecision.Rejected)\n            return ProposalDecision.Rejected;\n\n        // If any policy approves and none reject, approved.\n        if (first == ProposalDecision.Approved || second == ProposalDecision.Approved)\n            return ProposalDecision.Approved;\n\n        return ProposalDecision.Undecided;\n    }\n\n    #endregion\n\n    #region IDisposable\n\n    public void Dispose()\n    {\n        if (_disposed) return;\n\n        _lifecycleLock.Dispose();\n        _disposed = true;\n        GC.SuppressFinalize(this);\n    }\n\n    [MemberNotNull(nameof(_lifecycleLock))]\n    private void ThrowIfDisposed()\n    {\n        if (_disposed)\n            throw new ObjectDisposedException(nameof(GovernanceService));\n    }\n\n    #endregion\n}\n\n// ====================================================================================================================\n// Below are supporting types used by the GovernanceService. In a real-world project, each would live in its own file.\n// ====================================================================================================================\n\n#region Interfaces\n\n/// <summary>Public contract for interacting with on-chain governance.</summary>\npublic interface IGovernanceService\n{\n    Proposal CreateProposal(CreateProposalRequest request, CancellationToken ct = default);\n\n    VoteReceipt CastVote(Guid proposalId, VoteType voteType, string voterAddress, CancellationToken ct = default);\n\n    ProposalTally GetCurrentTally(Guid proposalId);\n\n    IEnumerable<Proposal> ListProposals(ProposalStatus? status = null);\n}\n\n/// <summary>\n///     Strategy interface for governance policy. private set; }\n\n    public bool HasVoted(string voterAddress) => _votes.ContainsKey(voterAddress);\n\n    public void AddVote(Vote vote)\n    {\n        if (!_votes.TryAdd(vote.VoterAddress, vote))\n            throw new GovernanceException(\"Duplicate vote detected.\");\n    }\n\n    public ProposalTally Tally()\n    {\n        var yes     = _votes.Values.Count(v => v.Type == VoteType.Yes);\n        var no      = _votes.Values.Count(v => v.Type == VoteType.No);\n        var abstain = _votes.Values.Count(v => v.Type == VoteType.Abstain);\n        return new ProposalTally(yes, no, abstain);\n    }\n\n    public void Finalize(DateTimeOffset finalizedAt, ProposalDecision decision)\n    {\n        Proposal = Proposal with\n        {\n            Status      = ProposalStatus.Finalized,\n            FinalizedAt = finalizedAt,\n            Decision    = decision\n        };\n    }\n}\n\n#endregion\n\n#region Policies\n\n/// <summary>\n///     Default simple-majority policy.\n///     Accepts proposal if YES > NO at the end of voting deadline.\n///     Requires a minimum turnout of 1 vote.\n/// </summary>\npublic sealed class SimpleMajorityPolicy : IGovernancePolicy\n{\n    public bool ValidateProposal(Proposal proposal)\n    {\n        if (proposal.VotingDeadline <= proposal.CreatedAt)\n            return false; // Deadline must be in the future\n\n        if (proposal.VotingDeadline - proposal.CreatedAt < TimeSpan.FromHours(1))\n            return false; // At least 1 hour voting window\n\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    public bool ValidateVote(Proposal proposal, Vote vote)\n    {\n        // Additional anti-sybil or stake-weight checks could be inserted here\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    public ProposalDecision EvaluateResult(Proposal proposal, ProposalTally tally)\n    {\n        if (tally.Total == 0)\n            return ProposalDecision.Rejected; // No participation → reject\n\n        return tally.YesCount > tally.NoCount\n            ? ProposalDecision.Approved\n            : ProposalDecision.Rejected;\n    }\n\n    public Task OnProposalFinalizedAsync(Proposal proposal, ProposalTally tally)\n    {\n        // Persistence or notifications can be handled here\n        return Task.CompletedTask;\n    }\n}\n\n#endregion\n\n#region Exceptions\n\npublic sealed class GovernanceException : Exception\n{\n    public GovernanceException(string message) : base(message) { }\n\n    public GovernanceException(string message, Exception inner) : base(message, inner) { }\n}\n\npublic sealed class ProposalNotFoundException : GovernanceException\n{\n    public ProposalNotFoundException(Guid proposalId)\n        : base($\"Proposal '{proposalId}' was not found.\") { }\n}\n\n#endregion\n```\n","numLines":440,"startLine":1,"totalLines":440}}}

async Task HandleNewConnectionAsync(\n        TcpClient client,\n        CancellationToken ct,\n        bool outboundInitiator = false)\n    {\n        var remoteEndPoint = Initiating shutdown.\",\n            e.Subsystem, e.Reason);\n\n        // Stop the host – triggers orderly shutdown.\n        _appLifetime.StopApplication();\n    }\n\n    public Task StopAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken)\n    {\n        _logger.LogInformation(\"Runtime coordinator stopping…\");\n        _bus.Unsubscribe<NodePanicEvent>(OnNodePanic);\n        return Task.CompletedTask;\n    }\n}\n\n/// <summary>\n/// Wraps another <see cref=\"IHostedService\"/> and retries it when it exits unexpectedly.\n/// This is an application of the proxy pattern providing resilience.\n/// </summary>\ninternal sealed class RetryingHostedServiceDecorator : IHostedService\n{\n    private readonly IHostedService _inner;\n    private readonly ILogger _logger;\n    private readonly CancellationTokenSource _cts = new();\n\n    public RetryingHostedServiceDecorator(IHostedService inner, ILoggerFactory loggerFactory)\n    {\n        _inner  = inner  ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(inner));\n        _logger = loggerFactory.CreateLogger(inner.GetType().Name + \"_RetryDecorator\");\n    }\n\n    public async Task StartAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken)\n    {\n        _logger.LogDebug(\"Starting hosted service with retry decorator: {Service}\", _inner.GetType().Name);\n\n        _ = Task.Run(() => RunWithRetryAsync(_cts.Token), CancellationToken.None);\n        await Task.CompletedTask;\n    }\n\n    private async Task RunWithRetryAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken)\n    {\n        const int maxAttempts = 5;\n        var attempt = 0;\n\n        while (!cancellationToken.IsCancellationRequested)\n        {\n            try\n            {\n                attempt++;\n                _logger.LogInformation(\"Attempt {Attempt} to start {Service}.\", attempt, _inner.GetType().Name);\n\n                await _inner.StartAsync(cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);\n                _logger.LogInformation(\"{Service} completed successfully.\", _inner.GetType().Name);\n                return; // exit loop\n            }\n            catch (Exception ex) when (attempt < maxAttempts)\n            {\n                _logger.LogWarning(ex,\n                    \"{Service} failed with error. Retrying in 5s ({Attempt}/{Max}).\",\n                    _inner.GetType().Name, attempt, maxAttempts);\n\n                await Task.Delay(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5), cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);\n            }\n            catch (Exception ex)\n            {\n                _logger.LogCritical(ex,\n                    \"{Service} failed after {MaxAttempts} attempts. Giving up.\",\n                    _inner.GetType().Name, maxAttempts);\n                throw;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    public async Task StopAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken)\n    {\n        _logger.LogDebug(\"Stopping hosted service with retry decorator: {Service}\", _inner.GetType().Name);\n\n        _cts.Cancel();\n        await _inner.StopAsync(cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);\n    }\n}\n\n/// <summary>\n/// Represents an event dispatched on unrecoverable failure of a subsystem.\n/// Listeners should shut down or transition to safe state.\n/// </summary>\npublic sealed record NodePanicEvent(string Subsystem, string Reason);\n\n/// <summary>\n/// Default implementation of a monotonic clock used for deterministic time handling\n/// across modules (critical for block timestamp validation).\n/// </summary>\ninternal sealed class SystemClock : IClock\n{\n    public DateTimeOffset UtcNow() => DateTimeOffset.UtcNow;\n}\n\n/// <summary>\n/// Provides strongly-typed configuration for the node host.\n/// </summary>\npublic sealed class NodeOptions\n{\n    public const string SectionName = \"Node\";\n    \n    /// <summary> Gets/sets the unique identifier of the network (E.g. 'EnergyNet'). </summary>\n    public required string NetworkId { get; init; }\n    \n    /// <summary> Path to data directory. </summary>\n    public required string DataDir { get; init; }\n\n    /// <summary> Indicates whether the REST / GraphQL gateway should be enabled. </summary>\n    public bool EnableApi { get; init; } = true;\n}\n```","numLines":362,"startLine":1,"totalLines":362}}}

  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    //  Slot Loop\n    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    private async Task RunSlotLoopAsync()\n    {\n        _logger.LogInformation(\"Slot loop started.\");\n        while (!_cts.IsCancellationRequested)\n        {\n            try\n            {\n                await    finally\n        {\n            _stateLock.ExitReadLock();\n        }\n\n        var slotStart   = _clock.UtcNow;\n        var slotEnd     = slotStart.Add(snapshot.SlotDuration);\n\n Implementations (Simplified Examples)\n\n    internal sealed class FungibleTokenStrategy : ITokenStandardStrategy\n    {\n        private readonly TokenDescriptor                        _descriptor;\n        private readonly ITokenRepository                       _repository;\n        private readonly IEventBus                              _eventBus;\n        private readonly ConcurrentDictionary<Address, ulong>   _balances = new();\n        private readonly ILogger<FungibleTokenStrategy>         _logger;\n\n        public FungibleTokenStrategy(\n            TokenDescriptor descriptor,\n            ITokenRepository repository,\n            IEventBus eventBus,\n            ILogger<FungibleTokenStrategy> logger)\n        {\n            _descriptor = descriptor;\n            _repository = repository;\n            _eventBus   = eventBus;\n            _logger     = logger;\n        }\n\n        public TokenDescriptor Descriptor => _descriptor;\n\n        public async Task MintAsync(Address to, ulong amount, CancellationToken ct)\n        {\n            _balances.AddOrUpdate(to, amount, (_, existing) => checked(existing + amount));\n            _eventBus.Publish(new TokenMintedEvent(_descriptor.TokenId, to, amount));\n            await Task.CompletedTask;\n        }\n\n        public async Task BurnAsync(Address from, ulong amount, CancellationToken ct)\n        {\n            if (!_balances.TryGetValue(from, out var current) || current < amount)\n                throw new TokenBalanceException(_descriptor.TokenId, from, current, amount);\n\n            _balances[from] = current - amount;\n            _eventBus.Publish(new TokenBurnedEvent(_descriptor.TokenId, from, amount));\n            await Task.CompletedTask;\n        }\n\n        public async Task TransferAsync(Address from, Address to, ulong amount, CancellationToken ct)\n        {\n            if (!_balances.TryGetValue(from, out var current) || current < amount)\n                throw new TokenBalanceException(_descriptor.TokenId, from, current, amount);\n\n            _balances[from] = current - amount;\n            _balances.AddOrUpdate(to, amount, (_, existing) => checked(existing + amount));\n            _eventBus.Publish(new TokenTransferredEvent(_descriptor.TokenId, from, to, amount));\n            await Task.CompletedTask;\n        }\n\n        public Task<ulong> GetBalanceAsync(Address address, CancellationToken ct)\n        {\n            _balances.TryGetValue(address, out var bal);\n            return Task.FromResult(bal);\n        }\n\n        public async Task CommitAsync()\n        {\n            foreach (var (addr, bal) in _balances)\n            {\n                await _repository.PersistBalanceAsync(_descriptor.TokenId, addr, bal, CancellationToken.None);\n            }\n        }\n\n        public void Dispose()\n        {\n            // Commit pending balances on dispose for safety.\n            CommitAsync().GetAwaiter().GetResult();\n        }\n    }\n\n    #endregion\n\n    #region Factory\n\n    internal sealed class TokenStandardFactory : ITokenStandardFactory\n    {\n        private     {\n            TokenDescriptor descriptor = new(id, standard, metadata);\n            return standard switch\n            {\n                TokenStandard.Fungible => new FungibleTokenStrategy(\n                    descriptor, _repository, _eventBus, \n                    _loggerFactory.CreateLogger<FungibleTokenStrategy>()),\n\n                // Additional standards (ERC721-like, soulbound, etc.) would be plugged in here.\n                _ => throw new NotSupportedException($\"Token standard '{standard}' is not supported.\")\n            };\n        }\n    }\n\n    #endregion\n\n Storage { get; } = new(StringComparer.Ordinal);\n\n        // Arbitrary data used by staking, governance or NFT modules. \n        // Kept intentionally loose for domain extension.\n        public Dictionary<string, object?> Metadata { get; } = new(StringComparer.Ordinal);\n\n        public object Clone()\n        {\n            var cloned = (AccountState)MemberwiseClone();\n            cloned.Storage = new(Storage, StringComparer.Ordinal);\n            cloned.Metadata = new(Storage.Count, StringComparer.Ordinal);\n\n            foreach (var (key, value) in Storage)\n                cloned.Storage[key] = (byte[])value.Clone();\n\n            foreach (var (key, value) in Metadata)\n                cloned.Metadata[key] = value switch\n                {\n                    ICloneable c => c.Clone(),\n                    _ => value\n                };\n\n            return cloned;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Declarative representation of an account modification, produced \n    /// by transaction execution engines.\n    /// </summary>\n    public sealed record AccountMutation(\n        string Address,\n        decimal BalanceDelta,\n        ulong? SetNonce = null,\n        IReadOnlyDictionary<string, byte[]>? StorageWrites = null,\n        IReadOnlyDictionary<string, object?>? MetadataWrites = null\n    );\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Public surface for state-machine interactions throughout the node.\n    /// </summary>\n    public interface IWorldState\n    {\n        AccountState GetAccount(string address, bool createIfMissing = false);\n        void Apply(AccountMutation mutation);\n        SnapshotToken TakeSnapshot();\n        void RevertSnapshot(SnapshotToken token);\n        ValueTask CommitAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);\n        byte[] ComputeRootHash();\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Thread-safe, in-memory implementation of <see cref=\"IWorldState\"/>.\n    /// Uses copy-on-write semantics for snapshots to minimise allocations.\n    /// In production, a persistent variant will write mutations to RocksDB/LMDB.\n    /// </summary>\n    public sealed class WorldState : IWorldState\n    {\n        private readonly ReaderWriterLockSlim _lock = new();\n        private readonly ILogger<WorldState> _logger;\n        private readonly IEventBus _eventBus;\n        private readonly IHashProvider _hashProvider;\n\n        // Canonical state – mutated after successful Commit().\n        private readonly Dictionary<string, AccountState> _accounts = new(StringComparer.Ordinal);\n        private long _version;\n\n        // Speculative layer applied on top of the canonical state until Commit/Revert.\n        private readonly Stack<StateSnapshot> _snapshots = new();\n\n        public WorldState(\n            ILogger<WorldState> logger,\n            IEventBus eventBus,\n            IHashProvider? hashProvider = null)\n        {\n            _logger = logger;\n            _eventBus = eventBus;\n            _hashProvider = hashProvider ?? new Sha256HashProvider();\n        }\n\n        #region Public API ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n        public AccountState GetAccount(string address, bool createIfMissing = false)\n        {\n            ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(address);\n\n            _lock.EnterReadLock();\n            try\n            {\n                if (CurrentLayer.TryGetValue(address, out var account))\n                    return account;\n\n                if (!createIfMissing)\n                    throw new KeyNotFoundException($\"Account '{address}' not found.\");\n\n                // Promote read-lock to write-lock\n                _lock.ExitReadLock();\n                _lock.EnterWriteLock();\n                try\n                {\n                    // Double-check to avoid race condition after lock promotion\n                    if (CurrentLayer.TryGetValue(address, out account))\n                        return account;\n\n                    account = new AccountState { Address = address };\n                    CurrentLayer[address] = account;\n                    return account;\n                }\n                finally\n                {\n                    _lock.ExitWriteLock();\n                    _lock.EnterReadLock(); // restore original lock state\n                }\n            }\n            finally\n            {\n                _lock.ExitReadLock();\n            }\n        }\n\n        public void Apply(AccountMutation mutation)\n        {\n            ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(mutation);\n\n            _lock.EnterWriteLock();\n            try\n            {\n                var target = GetAccount(mutation.Address, createIfMissing: true);\n\n                checked\n                {\n                    target.Balance += mutation.BalanceDelta;\n                }\n\n                if (mutation.SetNonce.HasValue)\n                    target.Nonce = mutation.SetNonce.Value;\n\n                if (mutation.StorageWrites is { Count: > 0 })\n                    foreach (var (key, value) in mutation.StorageWrites)\n                        target.Storage[key] = value;\n\n                if (mutation.MetadataWrites is { Count: > 0 })\n                    foreach (var (key, value) in mutation.MetadataWrites)\n                        target.Metadata[key] = value;\n            }\n            finally\n            {\n                _lock.ExitWriteLock();\n            }\n        }\n\n        public SnapshotToken TakeSnapshot()\n        {\n            _lock.EnterWriteLock();\n            try\n            {\n                var snapshot = new StateSnapshot(_version, ShallowClone(CurrentLayer));\n                _snapshots.Push(snapshot);\n                _logger.LogDebug(\"Snapshot taken at version {Version}\", snapshot.Version);\n                return new SnapshotToken(snapshot.Id, snapshot.Version);\n            }\n            finally\n            {\n                _lock.ExitWriteLock();\n            }\n        }\n\n        public void RevertSnapshot(SnapshotToken token)\n        {\n            _lock.EnterWriteLock();\n            try\n            {\n                if (_snapshots.Count == 0 || _snapshots.Peek().Id != token.Id)\n                    throw new InvalidOperationException(\"Snapshot mismatch or already reverted.\");\n\n                var snapshot = _snapshots.Pop();\n                RestoreLayer(snapshot.Layer);\n                _version = snapshot.Version;\n                _logger.LogWarning(\"State reverted to snapshot {Version}\", snapshot.Version);\n            }\n            finally\n            {\n                _lock.ExitWriteLock();\n            }\n        }\n\n        public async ValueTask CommitAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)\n        {\n            _lock.EnterWriteLock();\n            try\n            {\n                if (_snapshots.Count == 0)\n                    throw new InvalidOperationException(\"No snapshot to commit.\");\n\n                var snapshot = _snapshots.Pop();\n                // Merge snapshot layer into canonical layer\n                foreach (var (address, account) in snapshot.Layer)\n                    _accounts[address] = account;\n\n                _version++;\n                var root = ComputeRootHash();\n\n                // Publish event outside the lock to avoid dead-locks in user code\n                Task.Run(() =>\n                    _eventBus.Publish(new WorldStateCommittedEvent(root, _version)), cancellationToken);\n            }\n            finally\n            {\n                _lock.ExitWriteLock();\n            }\n\n            await Task.CompletedTask;\n        }\n\n        public byte[] ComputeRootHash()\n        {\n            _lock.EnterReadLock();\n            try\n            {\n                using var hashAlgo = IncrementalHash.CreateHash(HashAlgorithmName.SHA256);\n                foreach (var (address, account) in _accounts)\n                {\n                    hashAlgo.AppendData(System.Text.Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(address));\n                    hashAlgo.AppendData(BitConverter.GetBytes(account.Balance));\n                    hashAlgo.AppendData(BitConverter.GetBytes(account.Nonce));\n\n                    foreach (var (key, value) in account.Storage)\n                    {\n                        hashAlgo.AppendData(System.Text.Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(key));\n                        hashAlgo.AppendData(value);\n                    }\n                }\n                return hashAlgo.GetHashAndReset();\n            }\n            finally\n            {\n                _lock.ExitReadLock();\n            }\n        }\n\n        #endregion\n\n        #region Internal helpers ----------------------------------------------------------------\n\n        private Dictionary<string, AccountState> CurrentLayer =>\n            _snapshots.Count > 0 ? _snapshots.Peek().Layer : _accounts;\n\n        private static Dictionary<string, AccountState> ShallowClone(\n            Dictionary<string, AccountState> source)\n        {\n            var clone = new Dictionary<string, AccountState>(source.Count, StringComparer.Ordinal);\n            foreach (var (address, account) in source)\n                clone[address] = (AccountState)account.Clone();\n\n            return clone;\n        }\n\n        private void RestoreLayer(Dictionary<string, AccountState> target)\n        {\n            // Dispose modified accounts for GC friendliness in prod systems\n            _accounts.Clear();\n            foreach (var (address, account) in target)\n                _accounts[address] = account;\n        }\n\n        #endregion -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Internal representation for a snapshot, stored on a stack.\n    /// </summary>\n    internal sealed class StateSnapshot\n    {\n        public Guid Id { get; } = Guid.NewGuid();\n        public long Version { get; }\n        public Dictionary<string, AccountState> Layer { get; }\n\n        public StateSnapshot(long version, Dictionary<string, AccountState> layer)\n        {\n            Version = version;\n            Layer = layer;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Event raised after <see cref=\"WorldState.CommitAsync\"/> finishes successfully.\n    /// </summary>\n    /// <param name=\"RootHash\">Merkle-like hash of the global state.</param>\n    /// <param name=\"Version\">Monotonically increasing world-state version.</param>\n    public sealed record WorldStateCommittedEvent(byte[] RootHash, long Version);\n}\n```","numLines":371,"startLine":1,"totalLines":371}}}
