{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-5-20251101","id":"msg_01ArKhAZKmvrxUZcXtsJvzfa","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01WTbsaMjVSqrG53Vcvf34tU","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/logs/agent/solution.md","content":"# Technical Design Proposal: Migration from InMemoryEventBus to External Message Broker\n\n**Document ID**: TDP-2024-003\n**Status**: Draft\n**Author**: Solutions Architect\n**Date**: 2024\n**Target**: UtilityChain Core Suite\n\n---\n\n## Executive Summary\n\nThis document proposes a migration strategy for the UtilityChain Core Suite's inter-service communication architecture from the current synchronous, in-memory `InMemoryEventBus` to a persistent, external message broker. The goal is to address scalability bottlenecks, improve resilience, and enable independent service scaling as the platform prepares for mainnet launch with anticipated high transaction volumes and complex DeFi protocols.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Analysis of Current Architecture\n\n### 1.1 Current Event Bus Implementation\n\nThe UtilityChain Core Suite currently employs an event-driven architecture as documented in **ADR-003: Event-Driven Module Communication**. The implementation consists of:\n\n**Primary Interface** (`src/UtilityChain.Core/Services/InMemoryEventBus.cs:36-75`):\n```csharp\npublic interface IEventBus : IAsyncDisposable, IDisposable\n{\n    IDisposable Subscribe<TEvent>(\n        Func<TEvent, CancellationToken, ValueTask> handler,\n        string? subscriberId = null) where TEvent : IEvent;\n\n    ValueTask PublishAsync<TEvent>(\n        TEvent @event,\n        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default) where TEvent : IEvent;\n\n    bool HasSubscribers(Type eventType);\n}\n```\n\n**Implementation Characteristics** (`src/UtilityChain.Core/Services/InMemoryEventBus.cs:82-351`):\n- Uses `System.Threading.Channels` with a bounded capacity (default: 65,536 events)\n- Multiple dispatcher threads (defaults to `Environment.ProcessorCount`)\n- Fire-and-forget delivery model with Task.Run for each handler\n- Thread-safe subscription management via `ConcurrentDictionary`\n- FIFO ordering guarantee with built-in back-pressure\n\n### 1.2 Current Limitations\n\n| Limitation | Impact | Risk Level |\n|------------|--------|------------|\n| **Temporal Coupling** | All services must be running simultaneously for events to be delivered | High |\n| **Single Point of Failure** | If the bus crashes, all inter-module communication fails | Critical |\n| **No Durability** | Events are lost if the node crashes before handlers process them | Critical |\n| **No Horizontal Scaling** | Cannot scale individual services independently across nodes | High |\n| **No Guaranteed Delivery** | Fire-and-forget model means failed handlers lose events | High |\n| **Atomic Consistency Gap** | State changes and event publication are not transactionally atomic | Critical |\n\n---\n\n## 2. Affected Components (Publishers and Subscribers)\n\n### 2.1 Services Using IEventBus\n\nBased on comprehensive codebase analysis, the following services interact with `IEventBus`:\n\n| Service | File `TransactionAcceptedEvent`, `TransactionRejectedEvent` |\n| **StakingService** | `src/UtilityChain.Staking/StakingService.cs:31` | Publisher & Subscriber | Publishes: `StakeCreatedEvent`, `StakeWithdrawnEvent`, `RewardsClaimedEvent`; Subscribes: `NewBlockEvent` |\n| **GovernanceService** | `src/UtilityChain.Governance/GovernanceService.cs:36` | Publisher & Subscriber | Publishes: `ProposalCreatedEvent`, `VoteCastEvent`, `ProposalFinalizedEvent`; Subscribes: `ProposalCreatedEvent`, `ProposalFinalizedEvent` |\n| **ConsensusContext** | `src/UtilityChain.Consensus/ConsensusContext.cs:41` | Publisher & Subscriber | Publishes: `BlockProposedEvent`; Subscribes: `BlockCommittedEvent`, `VoteReceivedEvent` |\n| **TokenService** | `src/UtilityChain.Tokens/TokenService.cs:27` | Publisher & Subscriber | Publishes: `TokenCreatedEvent`, `TokenMintedEvent`, `TokenBurnedEvent`, `TokenTransferredEvent`; Subscribes: `BlockMinedEvent` |\n| **P2PService** | `src/UtilityChain.Networking/P2PService.cs:34` | Publisher | `PeerConnectedEvent`, `PeerDisconnectedEvent`, `NetworkMessageEvent` |\n| **WorldState** | `src/UtilityChain.Core/State/WorldState.cs:141` | Publisher | `WorldStateCommittedEvent` |\n| **GatewayController** | `src/UtilityChain.ApiGateway/GatewayController.cs:21` | Publisher | `TransactionReceived`, `ContractDeployed`, `ContractInvoked`, `BlockArrived` |\n| **NodeRuntimeCoordinator** | `src/UtilityChain.Node/Startup.cs:227` | Subscriber | `NodePanicEvent` |\n\n### 2.2 Event   │\n│      ├──▶ ContractDeployed                                                 │\n│      └──▶ BlockArrived                                                     │\n│                                                                             │\n│  TransactionProcessor                                                       │\n│      │                                                                      │\n│      ├──▶ TransactionAcceptedEvent ──▶ [Consensus, Indexer]                │\n│      └──▶ TransactionRejectedEvent                                         │\n│                                                                             │\n│  Consensus ◀─────────────────────┐                                         │\n│      │                           │                                          │\n│      ├──▶ BlockProposedEvent     │                                          │\n│      ├──▶ BlockCommittedEvent ───┼──▶ StakingService                       │\n│      │                           │                                          │\n│      └◀── VoteReceivedEvent ◀────┘                                         │\n│                                                                             │\n│  StakingService ◀── NewBlockEvent ◀── Consensus                            │\n│      │                                                                      │\n│      ├──▶ StakeCreatedEvent    │\n│                                                                             │\n│  GovernanceService                                                          │\n│      │                                                                      │\n│      ├──▶ ProposalCreatedEvent ──┐                                         │\n│      ├──▶ VoteCastEvent          │ (self-subscription for projections)     │\n│      └──▶ ProposalFinalizedEvent─┘                                         │\n│                                                                             │\n│  WorldState                                                                 │\n│      │                                                                      │\n│      └──▶ WorldStateCommittedEvent ──▶ [Snapshot Service, Indexer]         │\n│                                                                             │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. Proposed Solution: External Message Broker with Transactional Outbox\n\n### 3.1 Architecture Overview\n\nThe proposed architecture introduces:\n1. **External Message Broker** (RabbitMQ recommended for this use case)\n2. **Transactional Outbox Pattern** for atomic state+event persistence\n3. **Outbox Relay Service** for reliable event publication\n4. **Dead Letter Queues** for failed message handling\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                      PROPOSED ARCHITECTURE                                   │\n├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│                                                                             │\n│  ┌─────────────────┐                    ┌─────────────────┐                │\n│  │ TransactionProc │                    │   RabbitMQ      │                │\n│  │                 │                    │   Broker        │                │\n│  │  ┌───────────┐  │                    │  ┌───────────┐  │                │\n│  │  │ Business  │  │                    │  │ Exchange  │  │                │\n│  │  │ Logic     │  │                    │  │ (topic)   │  │                │\n│  │  └─────┬─────┘  │                    │  └─────┬─────┘  │                │\n│  │        │        │                    │        │        │                │\n│  │        ▼        │                    │        ▼        │                │\n│  │  ┌───────────┐  │                    │  ┌───────────┐  │                │\n│  │  │ DB + Out- │◀─┼────[Relay]────────▶│  │  Queues   │  │                │\n│  │  │ box Table │  │                    │  │           │  │                │\n│  │  └───────────┘  │                    │  └─────┬─────┘  │                │\n│  └─────────────────┘                    │        │        │                │\n│                                         │        ▼        │                │\n│  ┌─────────────────┐                    │  ┌───────────┐  │                │\n│  │ StakingService  │◀───────────────────┼──│ Consumer  │  │                │\n│  │                 │                    │  │ Groups    │  │                │\n│  └─────────────────┘                    │  └───────────┘  │                │\n│                                         │        │        │                │\n│  ┌─────────────────┐                    │        ▼        │                │\n│  │ GovernanceSvc   │◀───────────────────│  ┌───────────┐  │                │\n│  │                 │                    │  │   DLQ     │  │                │\n│  └─────────────────┘                    │  │ (Dead     │  │                │\n│                                         │  │  Letter)  │  │                │\n│                                         │  └───────────┘  │                │\n│                                         └─────────────────┘                │\n│                                                                             │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### 3.2 Addressing Asynchronous Consistency: The Transactional Outbox Pattern\n\n**Problem**: With an external broker, we cannot guarantee atomicity between:\n1. Persisting a state change (e.g., updating WorldState)\n2. Publishing the corresponding event to the broker\n\nIf we persist state but fail to publish, subscribers never learn of the change.\nIf we publish but fail to persist, we broadcast events for non-existent state.\n\n**Solution**: The **Transactional Outbox Pattern**\n\n#### 3.2.1 Outbox Table Schema\n\n```sql\nCREATE TABLE outbox_events (\n    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY,\n    event_type      VARCHAR(256) NOT NULL,\n    event_payload   JSONB NOT NULL,\n    created_at      TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),\n    published_at    TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE NULL,\n    retry_count     INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n    last_error      TEXT NULL,\n    correlation_id  VARCHAR(64) NULL,\n\n    INDEX idx_outbox_unpublished (published_at) WHERE published_at IS NULL\n);\n```\n\n#### 3.2.2 Modified Service Pattern (Example: TransactionProcessor)\n\n**Before** (`src/UtilityChain.TransactionProcessing/TransactionProcessor.cs:163`):\n```csharp\n// Current: Direct publish - NOT atomic with state change\nawait _eventBus.PublishAsync(new TransactionAcceptedEvent(tx), ct);\n```\n\n**After** (with Transactional Outbox):\n```csharp\npublic async Task HandleTransactionAsync(TransactionEnvelope envelope, CancellationToken ct)\n{\n    var tx = envelope.Transaction;\n\n    // Begin database transaction\n    await using var dbTransaction = await _dbContext.Database.BeginTransactionAsync(ct);\n\n    try\n    {\n        // 1. Persist to mempool (existing logic)\n        await _repository.AddPendingAsync(tx, ct);\n\n        // 2. Write event to outbox table (SAME transaction)\n        var outboxEvent = new OutboxEvent\n        {\n            Id = Guid.NewGuid(),\n            EventType = typeof(TransactionAcceptedEvent).AssemblyQualifiedName,\n            EventPayload = JsonSerializer.Serialize(new TransactionAcceptedEvent(tx)),\n            CreatedAt = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow,\n            CorrelationId = tx.Hash\n        };\n        _dbContext.OutboxEvents.Add(outboxEvent);\n\n        // 3. Commit both atomically\n        await _dbContext.SaveChangesAsync(ct);\n        await dbTransaction.CommitAsync(ct);\n\n        _logger.LogInformation(\"Transaction {TxHash} accepted and outbox event created.\", tx.Hash);\n    }\n    catch (Exception ex)\n    {\n        await dbTransaction.RollbackAsync(ct);\n        throw;\n    }\n}\n```\n\n#### 3.2.3 Outbox Relay Service\n\nA new background service polls the outbox and publishes to RabbitMQ:\n\n```csharp\n// src/UtilityChain.Core/Services/OutboxRelayService.cs\npublic sealed class OutboxRelayService : BackgroundService\n{\n    private readonly IDbContextFactory<UtilityChainDbContext> _dbFactory;\n    private readonly IMessageBroker _messageBroker;\n    private readonly ILogger<OutboxRelayService> _logger;\n    private readonly TimeSpan _pollingInterval = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(100);\n    private readonly int _batchSize = 100;\n\n    protected override async Task ExecuteAsync(CancellationToken stoppingToken)\n    {\n        while (!stoppingToken.IsCancellationRequested)\n        {\n            try\n            {\n                await using var dbContext = await _dbFactory.CreateDbContextAsync(stoppingToken);\n\n                var pendingEvents = await dbContext.OutboxEvents\n                    .Where(e => e.PublishedAt == null)\n                    .OrderBy(e => e.CreatedAt)\n                    .Take(_batchSize)\n                    .ToListAsync(stoppingToken);\n\n                foreach (var evt in pendingEvents)\n                {\n                    try\n                    {\n                        await _messageBroker.PublishAsync(\n                            evt.EventType,\n                            evt.EventPayload,\n                            evt.CorrelationId,\n                            stoppingToken);\n\n                        evt.PublishedAt = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow;\n                        await dbContext.SaveChangesAsync(stoppingToken);\n\n                        _logger.LogDebug(\"Published outbox event {EventId}\", evt.Id);\n                    }\n                    catch (Exception ex)\n                    {\n                        evt.RetryCount++;\n                        evt.LastError = ex.Message;\n                        await dbContext.SaveChangesAsync(stoppingToken);\n\n                        _logger.LogWarning(ex, \"Failed to publish outbox event {EventId}, retry {Retry}\",\n                            evt.Id, evt.RetryCount);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n            catch (Exception ex)\n            {\n                _logger.LogError(ex, \"Outbox relay error\");\n            }\n\n            await Task.Delay(_pollingInterval, stoppingToken);\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. Configuration and Dependency Management Changes\n\n### 4.1 Configuration File Changes\n\n**File**: `config/node-config.default.json`\n\nAdd new `messageBroker` section:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"services\": {\n    \"internalBus\": {\n      \"bufferCapacity\": 65536,\n      \"overflowStrategy\": \"DropOldest\"\n    },\n\n    \"messageBroker\": {\n      \"enabled\": true,\n      \"provider\": \"RabbitMQ\",\n      \"connection\": {\n        \"host\": \"localhost\",\n        \"port\": 5672,\n        \"virtualHost\": \"/utilitychain\",\n        \"username\": \"utilitychain\",\n        \"password\": \"${RABBITMQ_PASSWORD}\",\n        \"useSsl\": false,\n        \"sslCertPath\": \"\",\n        \"heartbeatIntervalSeconds\": 60,\n        \"connectionRetryCount\": 5,\n        \"connectionRetryDelayMs\": 3000\n      },\n      \"exchange\": {\n        \"name\": \"utilitychain.events\",\n        \"type\": \"topic\",\n        \"durable\": true,\n        \"autoDelete\": false\n      },\n      \"queues\": {\n        \"prefetchCount\": 10,\n        \"deadLetterExchange\": \"utilitychain.events.dlx\",\n        \"messageTtlMs\": 86400000\n      },\n      \"outbox\": {\n        \"enabled\": true,\n        \"pollingIntervalMs\": 100,\n        \"batchSize\": 100,\n        \"maxRetryCount\": 5,\n        \"retentionDays\": 7\n      },\n      \"resilience\": {\n        \"retryPolicy\": {\n          \"maxRetries\": 3,\n          \"initialDelayMs\": 100,\n          \"maxDelayMs\": 30000,\n          \"backoffMultiplier\": 2.0\n        },\n        \"circuitBreaker\": {\n          \"failureThreshold\": 5,\n          \"samplingDurationSeconds\": 30,\n          \"breakDurationSeconds\": 60\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### 4.2 Startup.cs Changes\n\n**File**: `src/UtilityChain.Node/Startup.cs`\n\n**Before** (line 101):\n```csharp\n// Infrastructure\nservices.AddSingleton<IEventBus, InMemoryEventBus>();\n```\n\n**After**:\n```csharp\n// Infrastructure - Event Bus\nvar messageBrokerConfig = ctx.Configuration.GetSection(\"services:messageBroker\");\nvar messageBrokerEnabled = messageBrokerConfig.GetValue<bool>(\"enabled\");\n\nif (messageBrokerEnabled)\n{\n    // External message broker (RabbitMQ)\n    services.Configure<MessageBrokerOptions>(messageBrokerConfig);\n    services.AddSingleton<IMessageBroker, RabbitMQMessageBroker>();\n    services.AddSingleton<IEventBus, ExternalEventBus>();\n    services.AddHostedService<OutboxRelayService>();\n\n    // Add health check for RabbitMQ\n    services.AddHealthChecks()\n            .AddRabbitMQ(sp => sp.GetRequiredService<IConnection>(),\n                         name: \"rabbitmq\",\n                         tags: new[] { \"ready\" });\n}\nelse\n{\n    // Fallback to in-memory for development/testing\n    services.AddSingleton<IEventBus, InMemoryEventBus>();\n}\n```\n\n### 4.3 New Abstractions and Implementations\n\n#### 4.3.1 IMessageBroker Interface\n\n```csharp\n// src/UtilityChain.Core/Abstractions/IMessageBroker.cs\nnamespace UtilityChain.Core.Abstractions;\n\npublic interface IMessageBroker : IAsyncDisposable\n{\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Publishes an event to the message broker.\n    /// </summary>\n    ValueTask PublishAsync<TEvent>(\n        TEvent @event,\n        string? correlationId = null,\n        CancellationToken ct = default) where TEvent : class, IEvent;\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Subscribes to events of a specific type.\n    /// </summary>\n    IDisposable Subscribe<TEvent>(\n        Func<TEvent, CancellationToken, ValueTask> handler,\n        string subscriptionId,\n        SubscriptionOptions? options = null) where TEvent : class, IEvent;\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Gets the health status of the broker connection.\n    /// </summary>\n    BrokerHealthStatus GetHealthStatus();\n}\n\npublic sealed record SubscriptionOptions\n{\n    public int PrefetchCount { get; init; } = 10;\n    public bool AutoAck { get; init; } = false;\n    public TimeSpan? AckTimeout { get; init; }\n    public string? DeadLetterQueue { get; init; }\n}\n\npublic enum BrokerHealthStatus\n{\n    Healthy,\n    Degraded,\n    Unhealthy\n}\n```\n\n#### 4.3.2 RabbitMQ Implementation (Partial)\n\n```csharp\n// src/UtilityChain.Core/Services/RabbitMQMessageBroker.cs\nnamespace UtilityChain.Core.Services;\n\npublic sealed class RabbitMQMessageBroker : IMessageBroker\n{\n    private readonly IConnection _connection;\n    private readonly IModel _publishChannel;\n    private readonly ILogger<RabbitMQMessageBroker> _logger;\n    private readonly MessageBrokerOptions _options;\n    private readonly AsyncPolicy _retryPolicy;\n    private readonly ConcurrentDictionary<string, IModel> _consumerChannels = new();\n\n    public RabbitMQMessageBroker(\n        IOptions<MessageBrokerOptions> options,\n        ILogger<RabbitMQMessageBroker> logger)\n    {\n        _options = options.Value;\n        _logger = logger;\n\n        // Build resilience policy using Polly\n        _retryPolicy = Policy\n            .Handle<BrokerUnreachableException>()\n            .Or<SocketException>()\n            .WaitAndRetryAsync(\n                _options.Resilience.RetryPolicy.MaxRetries,\n                retryAttempt => TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(\n                    Math.Min(\n                        _options.Resilience.RetryPolicy.InitialDelayMs *\n                        Math.Pow(_options.Resilience.RetryPolicy.BackoffMultiplier, retryAttempt),\n                        _options.Resilience.RetryPolicy.MaxDelayMs)),\n                (ex, timeSpan, retryCount, ctx) =>\n                {\n                    _logger.LogWarning(ex,\n                        \"RabbitMQ connection failed. Retry {RetryCount} in {Delay}ms\",\n                        retryCount, timeSpan.TotalMilliseconds);\n                });\n\n        _connection = CreateConnection();\n        _publishChannel = _connection.CreateModel();\n\n        DeclareTopology();\n    }\n\n    private IConnection CreateConnection()\n    {\n        var factory = new ConnectionFactory\n        {\n            HostName = _options.Connection.Host,\n            Port = _options.Connection.Port,\n            VirtualHost = _options.Connection.VirtualHost,\n            UserName = _options.Connection.Username,\n            Password = _options.Connection.Password,\n            RequestedHeartbeat = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(_options.Connection.HeartbeatIntervalSeconds),\n            AutomaticRecoveryEnabled = true,\n            NetworkRecoveryInterval = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(10),\n            DispatchConsumersAsync = true\n        };\n\n        return factory.CreateConnection();\n    }\n\n    private void DeclareTopology()\n    {\n        // Main exchange\n        _publishChannel.ExchangeDeclare(\n            exchange: _options.Exchange.Name,\n            type: _options.Exchange.Type,\n            durable: _options.Exchange.Durable,\n            autoDelete: _options.Exchange.AutoDelete);\n\n        // Dead letter exchange\n        _publishChannel.ExchangeDeclare(\n            exchange: _options.Queues.DeadLetterExchange,\n            type: ExchangeType.Direct,\n            durable: true);\n    }\n\n    public async ValueTask PublishAsync<TEvent>(\n        TEvent @event,\n        string? correlationId = null,\n        CancellationToken ct = default) where TEvent : class, IEvent\n    {\n        var routingKey = GetRoutingKey<TEvent>();\n        var body = JsonSerializer.SerializeToUtf8Bytes(@event);\n\n        var properties = _publishChannel.CreateBasicProperties();\n        properties.ContentType = \"application/json\";\n        properties.DeliveryMode = 2; // persistent\n        properties.MessageId = Guid.NewGuid().ToString();\n        properties.Timestamp = new AmqpTimestamp(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.ToUnixTimeSeconds());\n        properties.Type = typeof(TEvent).FullName;\n\n        if (correlationId != null)\n            properties.CorrelationId = correlationId;\n\n        await _retryPolicy.ExecuteAsync(async () =>\n        {\n            _publishChannel.BasicPublish(\n                exchange: _options.Exchange.Name,\n                routingKey: routingKey,\n                basicProperties: properties,\n                body: body);\n\n            _logger.LogDebug(\"Published {EventType} to {RoutingKey}\",\n                typeof(TEvent).Name, routingKey);\n        });\n    }\n\n    public IDisposable Subscribe<TEvent>(\n        Func<TEvent, CancellationToken, ValueTask> handler,\n        string subscriptionId,\n        SubscriptionOptions? options = null) where TEvent : class, IEvent\n    {\n        options ??= new SubscriptionOptions();\n        var queueName = $\"utilitychain.{typeof(TEvent).Name}.{subscriptionId}\";\n        var routingKey = GetRoutingKey<TEvent>();\n\n        var channel = _connection.CreateModel();\n        _consumerChannels[queueName] = channel;\n\n        // Declare queue with DLQ\n        var queueArgs = new Dictionary<string, object>\n        {\n            [\"x-dead-letter-exchange\"] = _options.Queues.DeadLetterExchange,\n            [\"x-dead-letter-routing-key\"] = $\"dlq.{queueName}\"\n        };\n\n        channel.QueueDeclare(\n            queue: queueName,\n            durable: true,\n            exclusive: false,\n            autoDelete: false,\n            arguments: queueArgs);\n\n        channel.QueueBind(queueName, _options.Exchange.Name, routingKey);\n        channel.BasicQos(0, (ushort)options.PrefetchCount, false);\n\n        var consumer = new AsyncEventingBasicConsumer(channel);\n        consumer.Received += async (_, ea) =>\n        {\n            try\n            {\n                var @event = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<TEvent>(ea.Body.Span)!;\n                await handler(@event, CancellationToken.None);\n                channel.BasicAck(ea.DeliveryTag, false);\n            }\n            catch (Exception ex)\n            {\n                _logger.LogError(ex, \"Error processing {EventType}\", typeof(TEvent).Name);\n                channel.BasicNack(ea.DeliveryTag, false, false); // Send to DLQ\n            }\n        };\n\n        channel.BasicConsume(queueName, false, consumer);\n\n        return new SubscriptionHandle(() =>\n        {\n            channel.Close();\n            _consumerChannels.TryRemove(queueName, out _);\n        });\n    }\n\n    private static string GetRoutingKey<TEvent>() => typeof(TEvent).FullName!.ToLowerInvariant();\n\n    public BrokerHealthStatus GetHealthStatus()\n    {\n        return _connection.IsOpen ? BrokerHealthStatus.Healthy : BrokerHealthStatus.Unhealthy;\n    }\n\n    public async ValueTask DisposeAsync()\n    {\n        foreach (var channel in _consumerChannels.Values)\n            channel.Close();\n\n        _publishChannel.Close();\n        _connection.Close();\n    }\n\n    private sealed class SubscriptionHandle : IDisposable\n    {\n        private readonly Action _disposeAction;\n        public SubscriptionHandle(Action disposeAction) => _disposeAction = disposeAction;\n        public void Dispose() => _disposeAction();\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Resilience and Error Handling Strategy\n\n### 5.1 New Failure Modes Introduced\n\n| Failure Mode | Description | Mitigation |\n|--------------|-------------|------------|\n| **Network Partition** | Node loses connectivity to broker | Circuit breaker + local buffering |\n| **Broker Downtime** | RabbitMQ cluster unavailable | Outbox pattern ensures eventual delivery |\n| **Poison Messages** | Messages that repeatedly fail processing | Dead Letter Queue + manual intervention |\n| **Consumer Lag** | Subscribers can't keep up with producers | Backpressure, scaling consumers |\n| **Message Expiry** | TTL exceeded before processing | Alerting, DLQ inspection |\n| **Duplicate Delivery** | At-least-once semantics can cause duplicates | Idempotent handlers (deduplication) |\n\n### 5.2 Dead Letter Queue (DLQ) Strategy\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                    DLQ PROCESSING FLOW                          │\n├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│                                                                 │\n│  Normal Queue                                                   │\n│      │                                                          │\n│      ├──▶ Handler Success ──▶ ACK ──▶ Remove from Queue        │\n│      │                                                          │\n│      └──▶ Handler Failure ──▶ NACK ──▶ DLQ                     │\n│                                         │                       │\n│                                         ▼                       │\n│                               ┌─────────────────┐              │\n│                               │  Dead Letter    │              │\n│                               │  Queue (DLQ)    │              │\n│                               └────────┬────────┘              │\n│                                        │                        │\n│                     ┌──────────────────┼──────────────────┐    │\n│                     ▼                  ▼                  ▼    │\n│              ┌──────────┐      ┌──────────┐      ┌──────────┐ │\n│              │ Alert    │      │ Manual   │      │ Requeue  │ │\n│              │ Ops Team │      │ Inspect  │      │ w/ Delay │ │\n│              └──────────┘      └──────────┘      └──────────┘ │\n│                                                                 │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### 5.3 Retry Policy Configuration (Polly)\n\n```csharp\n// Applied to both publishing and consuming\nservices.AddSingleton<IAsyncPolicy>(sp =>\n{\n    var options = sp.GetRequiredService<IOptions<MessageBrokerOptions>>().Value;\n\n    var retryPolicy = Policy\n        .Handle<BrokerUnreachableException>()\n        .Or<AlreadyClosedException>()\n        .WaitAndRetryAsync(\n            options.Resilience.RetryPolicy.MaxRetries,\n            retryAttempt => TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(\n                options.Resilience.RetryPolicy.InitialDelayMs *\n                Math.Pow(options.Resilience.RetryPolicy.BackoffMultiplier, retryAttempt)));\n\n    var circuitBreaker = Policy\n        .Handle<BrokerUnreachableException>()\n        .CircuitBreakerAsync(\n            exceptionsAllowedBeforeBreaking: options.Resilience.CircuitBreaker.FailureThreshold,\n            durationOfBreak: TimeSpan.FromSeconds(options.Resilience.CircuitBreaker.BreakDurationSeconds),\n            onBreak: (ex, duration) =>\n                Log.Warning(\"Circuit breaker opened for {Duration}s due to {Exception}\",\n                           duration.TotalSeconds, ex.GetType().Name),\n            onReset: () => Log.Information(\"Circuit breaker reset\"),\n            onHalfOpen: () => Log.Information(\"Circuit breaker half-open\"));\n\n    return Policy.WrapAsync(retryPolicy, circuitBreaker);\n});\n```\n\n### 5.4 Idempotent Handler Pattern\n\nTo handle duplicate message delivery:\n\n```csharp\npublic class IdempotentEventHandler<TEvent> : IEventHandler<TEvent>\n    where TEvent : class, IEvent\n{\n    private readonly IEventHandler<TEvent> _inner;\n    private readonly IProcessedEventStore _processedStore;\n    private readonly ILogger _logger;\n\n    public async ValueTask HandleAsync(TEvent @event, CancellationToken ct)\n    {\n        var eventId = GetEventId(@event);\n\n        if (await _processedStore.HasBeenProcessedAsync(eventId, ct))\n        {\n            _logger.LogDebug(\"Event {EventId} already processed, skipping\", eventId);\n            return;\n        }\n\n        await _inner.HandleAsync(@event, ct);\n        await _processedStore.MarkAsProcessedAsync(eventId, ct);\n    }\n\n    private static string GetEventId(TEvent @event) =>\n        @event switch\n        {\n            IHasEventId e => e.EventId.ToString(),\n            _ => @event.GetHashCode().ToString()\n        };\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6. Architectural Trade-offs Analysis\n\n### 6.1 Advantages (Pros)\n\n| Benefit | Description | Impact |\n|---------|-------------|--------|\n| **Horizontal Scalability** | Services can be scaled independently; multiple consumers can share workload | High |\n| **Temporal Decoupling** | Publishers and subscribers don't need to be online simultaneously | High |\n| **Durability** | Messages persist across restarts; no data loss on crashes | Critical |\n| **Guaranteed Delivery** | At-least-once semantics with acknowledgments | High |\n| **Fault Isolation** | Broker failure doesn't crash the node; local buffering via outbox | High |\n| **Observability** | Message broker provides metrics, tracing, queue depths | Medium |\n| **Independent Deployments** | Services can be updated without coordinated deployments | Medium |\n| **Load Leveling** | Spiky workloads absorbed by queue; consumers process at their own pace | Medium |\n\n### 6.2 Disadvantages (Cons)\n\n| Drawback | Description | Mitigation |\n|----------|-------------|------------|\n| **Operational Complexity** | Additional infrastructure (RabbitMQ cluster) to manage | Use managed service (CloudAMQP, Amazon MQ) |\n| **Increased Latency** | Network hop + serialization adds ~1-10ms per event | Acceptable for most use cases; keep in-memory for latency-critical paths |\n| **Eventual Consistency** | Subscribers see state changes with delay | Design for eventual consistency; use compensating transactions |\n| **Debugging Difficulty** | Distributed tracing required; harder to follow event flow | Implement correlation IDs, structured logging, distributed tracing (Jaeger) |\n| **Message Ordering** | Global ordering not guaranteed across partitions | Use single partition for strict ordering; design for out-of-order delivery |\n| **Duplicate Handling** | At-least-once semantics requires idempotent handlers | Implement deduplication at handler level |\n| **Infrastructure Cost** | Additional servers, storage, network | Cost justified by resilience gains |\n| **Schema Evolution** | Event payload changes require versioning strategy | Use schema registry; forward-compatible serialization |\n\n### 6.3 Risk Assessment Matrix\n\n| Risk | Probability | Impact | Mitigation |\n|------|-------------|--------|------------|\n| Broker cluster failure | Low | Critical | Multi-AZ deployment, automatic failover |\n| Message backlog overflow | Medium | High | Queue size limits, alerting, backpressure |\n| Poison message loops | Medium | Medium | DLQ with TTL, manual inspection |\n| Schema incompatibility | Medium | High | Versioned schemas, compatibility testing |\n| Network latency spikes | Medium | Medium | Timeouts, circuit breakers |\n\n---\n\n## 7. Migration Plan\n\n### 7.1 Phased Rollout\n\n| Phase | Description | Duration | Rollback Plan |\n|-------|-------------|----------|---------------|\n| **Phase 1** | Deploy RabbitMQ cluster; add configuration | 1 week | Remove config |\n| **Phase 2** | Implement dual-write (in-memory + outbox) | 2 weeks | Disable outbox |\n| **Phase 3** | Deploy outbox relay service | 1 week | Stop relay service |\n| **Phase 4** | Migrate non-critical events (telemetry, logging) | 2 weeks | Feature flag |\n| **Phase 5** | Migrate critical events (consensus, transactions) | 3 weeks | Feature flag |\n| **Phase 6** | Remove in-memory bus; full external | 1 week | Re-enable in-memory |\n\n### 7.2 Feature Flags\n\n```json\n{\n  \"featureFlags\": {\n    \"useExternalBroker\": false,\n    \"externalBrokerEvents\": [\n      \"TransactionAcceptedEvent\",\n      \"TransactionRejectedEvent\"\n    ],\n    \"dualWriteEnabled\": true\n  }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 8. Conclusion\n\nThe migration from `InMemoryEventBus` to an external message broker with the Transactional Outbox pattern addresses critical scalability and resilience limitations in the UtilityChain Core Suite. While introducing operational complexity, the benefits—particularly guaranteed delivery, temporal decoupling, and horizontal scalability—are essential for mainnet readiness with high transaction volumes and complex DeFi protocols.\n\n**Recommended Next Steps**:\n1. Approve this design proposal\n2. Provision RabbitMQ cluster in staging environment\n3. Implement IMessageBroker abstraction and RabbitMQ adapter\n4. Add outbox table to database schema\n5. Modify TransactionProcessor as proof-of-concept\n6. Execute phased migration plan\n\n---\n\n## Appendix A: Files Requiring Modification\n\n| File | Change Type | Description |\n|------|-------------|-------------|\n| `config/node-config.default.json` | The goal is to address scalability bottlenecks, improve resilience, and enable independent service scaling as the platform prepares for mainnet launch with anticipated high transaction volumes and complex DeFi protocols.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Analysis of Current Architecture\n\n### 1.1 Current Event Bus Implementation\n\nThe UtilityChain Core Suite currently employs an event-driven architecture as documented in **ADR-003: Event-Driven Module Communication**. The implementation consists of:\n\n**Primary Interface** (`src/UtilityChain.Core/Services/InMemoryEventBus.cs:36-75`):\n```csharp\npublic interface IEventBus : IAsyncDisposable, IDisposable\n{\n    IDisposable Subscribe<TEvent>(\n        Func<TEvent, CancellationToken, ValueTask> handler,\n        string? subscriberId = null) where TEvent : IEvent;\n\n    ValueTask PublishAsync<TEvent>(\n        TEvent @event,\n        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default) where TEvent : IEvent;\n\n    bool HasSubscribers(Type eventType);\n}\n```\n\n**Implementation Characteristics** (`src/UtilityChain.Core/Services/InMemoryEventBus.cs:82-351`):\n- Uses `System.Threading.Channels` with a bounded capacity (default: 65,536 events)\n- Multiple dispatcher threads (defaults to `Environment.ProcessorCount`)\n- Fire-and-forget delivery model with Task.Run for each handler\n- Thread-safe subscription management via `ConcurrentDictionary`\n- FIFO ordering guarantee with built-in back-pressure\n\n### 1.2 Current Limitations\n\n| Limitation | Impact | Risk Level |\n|------------|--------|------------|\n| **Temporal Coupling** | All services must be running simultaneously for events to be delivered | High |\n| **Single Point of Failure** | If the bus crashes, all inter-module communication fails | Critical |\n| **No Durability** | Events are lost if the node crashes before handlers process them | Critical |\n| **No Horizontal Scaling** | Cannot scale individual services independently across nodes | High |\n| **No Guaranteed Delivery** | Fire-and-forget model means failed handlers lose events | High |\n| **Atomic Consistency Gap** | State changes and event publication are not transactionally atomic | Critical |\n\n---\n\n## 2. Affected Components (Publishers and Subscribers)\n\n### 2.1 Services Using IEventBus\n\nBased on comprehensive codebase analysis, the following services interact with `IEventBus`:\n\n| Service | File Location | Role | Events |\n|---------|---------------|------|--------|\n| **TransactionProcessor** | `src/UtilityChain.TransactionProcessing/TransactionProcessor.cs:29` | Publisher | `TransactionAcceptedEvent`, `TransactionRejectedEvent` |\n| **StakingService** | `src/UtilityChain.Staking/StakingService.cs:31` | Publisher & Subscriber | Publishes: `StakeCreatedEvent`, `StakeWithdrawnEvent`, `RewardsClaimedEvent`; Subscribes: `NewBlockEvent` |\n| **GovernanceService** | `src/UtilityChain.Governance/GovernanceService.cs:36` | Publisher & Subscriber | Publishes: `ProposalCreatedEvent`, `VoteCastEvent`, `ProposalFinalizedEvent`; Subscribes: `ProposalCreatedEvent`, `ProposalFinalizedEvent` |\n| **ConsensusContext** | `src/UtilityChain.Consensus/ConsensusContext.cs:41` | Publisher & Subscriber | Publishes: `BlockProposedEvent`; Subscribes: `BlockCommittedEvent`, `VoteReceivedEvent` |\n| **TokenService** | `src/UtilityChain.Tokens/TokenService.cs:27` | Publisher & Subscriber | Publishes: `TokenCreatedEvent`, `TokenMintedEvent`, `TokenBurnedEvent`, `TokenTransferredEvent`; Subscribes: `BlockMinedEvent` |\n| **P2PService** | `src/UtilityChain.Networking/P2PService.cs:34` | Publisher | `PeerConnectedEvent`, `PeerDisconnectedEvent`, `NetworkMessageEvent` |\n| **WorldState** | `src/UtilityChain.Core/State/WorldState.cs:141` | Publisher | `WorldStateCommittedEvent` |\n| **GatewayController** | `src/UtilityChain.ApiGateway/GatewayController.cs:21` | Publisher | `TransactionReceived`, `ContractDeployed`, `ContractInvoked`, `BlockArrived` |\n| **NodeRuntimeCoordinator** | `src/UtilityChain.Node/Startup.cs:227` | Subscriber | `NodePanicEvent` |\n\n### 2.2 Event Flow Diagram\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                        EVENT PUBLICATION PATTERNS                            │\n├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│                                                                             │\n│  API Gateway                                                                │\n│      │                                                                      │\n│      ├──▶ TransactionReceived ──▶ [External Clients via SSE]               │\n│      ├──▶ ContractDeployed                                                 │\n│      └──▶ BlockArrived                                                     │\n│                                                                             │\n│  TransactionProcessor                                                       │\n│      │                                                                      │\n│      ├──▶ TransactionAcceptedEvent ──▶ [Consensus, Indexer]                │\n│      └──▶ TransactionRejectedEvent                                         │\n│                                                                             │\n│  Consensus ◀─────────────────────┐                                         │\n│      │                           │                                          │\n│      ├──▶ BlockProposedEvent     │                                          │\n│      ├──▶ BlockCommittedEvent ───┼──▶ StakingService                       │\n│      │                           │                                          │\n│      └◀── VoteReceivedEvent ◀────┘                                         │\n│                                                                             │\n│  StakingService ◀── NewBlockEvent ◀── Consensus                            │\n│      │                                                                      │\n│      ├──▶ StakeCreatedEvent                                                │\n│      ├──▶ StakeWithdrawnEvent                                              │\n│      └──▶ RewardsClaimedEvent                                              │\n│                                                                             │\n│  GovernanceService                                                          │\n│      │                                                                      │\n│      ├──▶ ProposalCreatedEvent ──┐                                         │\n│      ├──▶ VoteCastEvent          │ (self-subscription for projections)     │\n│      └──▶ ProposalFinalizedEvent─┘                                         │\n│                                                                             │\n│  WorldState                                                                 │\n│      │                                                                      │\n│      └──▶ WorldStateCommittedEvent ──▶ [Snapshot Service, Indexer]         │\n│                                                                             │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. Proposed Solution: External Message Broker with Transactional Outbox\n\n### 3.1 Architecture Overview\n\nThe proposed architecture introduces:\n1. **External Message Broker** (RabbitMQ recommended for this use case)\n2. **Transactional Outbox Pattern** for atomic state+event persistence\n3. **Outbox Relay Service** for reliable event publication\n4. **Dead Letter Queues** for failed message handling\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                      PROPOSED ARCHITECTURE                                   │\n├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│                                                                             │\n│  ┌─────────────────┐                    ┌─────────────────┐                │\n│  │ TransactionProc │                    │   RabbitMQ      │                │\n│  │                 │                    │   Broker        │                │\n│  │  ┌───────────┐  │                    │  ┌───────────┐  │                │\n│  │  │ Business  │  │                    │  │ Exchange  │  │                │\n│  │  │ Logic     │  │                    │  │ (topic)   │  │                │\n│  │  └─────┬─────┘  │                    │  └─────┬─────┘  │                │\n│  │        │        │                    │        │        │                │\n│  │        ▼        │                    │        ▼        │                │\n│  │  ┌───────────┐  │                    │  ┌───────────┐  │                │\n│  │  │ DB + Out- │◀─┼────[Relay]────────▶│  │  Queues   │  │                │\n│  │  │ box Table │  │                    │  │           │  │                │\n│  │  └───────────┘  │                    │  └─────┬─────┘  │                │\n│  └─────────────────┘                    │        │        │                │\n│                                         │        ▼        │                │\n│  ┌─────────────────┐                    │  ┌───────────┐  │                │\n│  │ StakingService  │◀───────────────────┼──│ Consumer  │  │                │\n│  │                 │                    │  │ Groups    │  │                │\n│  └─────────────────┘                    │  └───────────┘  │                │\n│                                         │        │        │                │\n│  ┌─────────────────┐                    │        ▼        │                │\n│  │ GovernanceSvc   │◀───────────────────│  ┌───────────┐  │                │\n│  │                 │                    │  │   DLQ     │  │                │\n│  └─────────────────┘                    │  │ (Dead     │  │                │\n│                                         │  │  Letter)  │  │                │\n│                                         │  └───────────┘  │                │\n│                                         └─────────────────┘                │\n│                                                                             │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### 3.2 Addressing Asynchronous Consistency: The Transactional Outbox Pattern\n\n**Problem**: With an external broker, we cannot guarantee atomicity between:\n1. Persisting a state change (e.g., updating WorldState)\n2. Publishing the corresponding event to the broker\n\nIf we persist state but fail to publish, subscribers never learn of the change.\nIf we publish but fail to persist, we broadcast events for non-existent state.\n\n**Solution**: The **Transactional Outbox Pattern**\n\n#### 3.2.1 Outbox Table Schema\n\n```sql\nCREATE TABLE outbox_events (\n    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY,\n    event_type      VARCHAR(256) NOT NULL,\n    event_payload   JSONB NOT NULL,\n    created_at      TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),\n    published_at    TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE NULL,\n    retry_count     INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n    last_error      TEXT NULL,\n    correlation_id  VARCHAR(64) NULL,\n\n    INDEX idx_outbox_unpublished (published_at) WHERE published_at IS NULL\n);\n```\n\n#### 3.2.2 Modified Service Pattern (Example: TransactionProcessor)\n\n**Before** (`src/UtilityChain.TransactionProcessing/TransactionProcessor.cs:163`):\n```csharp\n// Current: Direct publish - NOT atomic with state change\nawait _eventBus.PublishAsync(new TransactionAcceptedEvent(tx), ct);\n```\n\n**After** (with Transactional Outbox):\n```csharp\npublic async Task HandleTransactionAsync(TransactionEnvelope envelope, CancellationToken ct)\n{\n    var tx = envelope.Transaction;\n\n    // Begin database transaction\n    await using var dbTransaction = await _dbContext.Database.BeginTransactionAsync(ct);\n\n    try\n    {\n        // 1. Persist to mempool (existing logic)\n        await _repository.AddPendingAsync(tx, ct);\n\n        // 2. Write event to outbox table (SAME transaction)\n        var outboxEvent = new OutboxEvent\n        {\n            Id = Guid.NewGuid(),\n            EventType = typeof(TransactionAcceptedEvent).AssemblyQualifiedName,\n            EventPayload = JsonSerializer.Serialize(new TransactionAcceptedEvent(tx)),\n            CreatedAt = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow,\n            CorrelationId = tx.Hash\n        };\n        _dbContext.OutboxEvents.Add(outboxEvent);\n\n        // 3. Commit both atomically\n        await _dbContext.SaveChangesAsync(ct);\n        await dbTransaction.CommitAsync(ct);\n\n        _logger.LogInformation(\"Transaction {TxHash} accepted and outbox event created.\", tx.Hash);\n    }\n    catch (Exception ex)\n    {\n        await dbTransaction.RollbackAsync(ct);\n        throw;\n    }\n}\n```\n\n#### 3.2.3 Outbox Relay Service\n\nA new background service polls the outbox and publishes to RabbitMQ:\n\n```csharp\n// src/UtilityChain.Core/Services/OutboxRelayService.cs\npublic sealed class OutboxRelayService : BackgroundService\n{\n    private readonly IDbContextFactory<UtilityChainDbContext> _dbFactory;\n    private readonly IMessageBroker _messageBroker;\n    private readonly ILogger<OutboxRelayService> _logger;\n    private readonly TimeSpan _pollingInterval = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(100);\n    private readonly int _batchSize = 100;\n\n    protected override async Task ExecuteAsync(CancellationToken stoppingToken)\n    {\n        while (!stoppingToken.IsCancellationRequested)\n        {\n            try\n            {\n                await using var dbContext = await _dbFactory.CreateDbContextAsync(stoppingToken);\n\n                var pendingEvents = await dbContext.OutboxEvents\n                    .Where(e => e.PublishedAt == null)\n                    .OrderBy(e => e.CreatedAt)\n                    .Take(_batchSize)\n                    .ToListAsync(stoppingToken);\n\n                foreach (var evt in pendingEvents)\n                {\n                    try\n                    {\n                        await _messageBroker.PublishAsync(\n                            evt.EventType,\n                            evt.EventPayload,\n                            evt.CorrelationId,\n                            stoppingToken);\n\n                        evt.PublishedAt = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow;\n                        await dbContext.SaveChangesAsync(stoppingToken);\n\n                        _logger.LogDebug(\"Published outbox event {EventId}\", evt.Id);\n                    }\n                    catch (Exception ex)\n                    {\n                        evt.RetryCount++;\n                        evt.LastError = ex.Message;\n                        await dbContext.SaveChangesAsync(stoppingToken);\n\n                        _logger.LogWarning(ex, \"Failed to publish outbox event {EventId}, retry {Retry}\",\n                            evt.Id, evt.RetryCount);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n            catch (Exception ex)\n            {\n                _logger.LogError(ex, \"Outbox relay error\");\n            }\n\n            await Task.Delay(_pollingInterval, stoppingToken);\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. Configuration and Dependency Management Changes\n\n### 4.1 Configuration File Changes\n\n**File**: `config/node-config.default.json`\n\nAdd new `messageBroker` section:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"services\": {\n    \"internalBus\": {\n      \"bufferCapacity\": 65536,\n      \"overflowStrategy\": \"DropOldest\"\n    },\n\n    \"messageBroker\": {\n      \"enabled\": true,\n      \"provider\": \"RabbitMQ\",\n      \"connection\": {\n        \"host\": \"localhost\",\n        \"port\": 5672,\n        \"virtualHost\": \"/utilitychain\",\n        \"username\": \"utilitychain\",\n        \"password\": \"${RABBITMQ_PASSWORD}\",\n        \"useSsl\": false,\n        \"sslCertPath\": \"\",\n        \"heartbeatIntervalSeconds\": 60,\n        \"connectionRetryCount\": 5,\n        \"connectionRetryDelayMs\": 3000\n      },\n      \"exchange\": {\n        \"name\": \"utilitychain.events\",\n        \"type\": \"topic\",\n        \"durable\": true,\n        \"autoDelete\": false\n      },\n      \"queues\": {\n        \"prefetchCount\": 10,\n        \"deadLetterExchange\": \"utilitychain.events.dlx\",\n        \"messageTtlMs\": 86400000\n      },\n      \"outbox\": {\n        \"enabled\": true,\n        \"pollingIntervalMs\": 100,\n        \"batchSize\": 100,\n        \"maxRetryCount\": 5,\n        \"retentionDays\": 7\n      },\n      \"resilience\": {\n        \"retryPolicy\": {\n          \"maxRetries\": 3,\n          \"initialDelayMs\": 100,\n          \"maxDelayMs\": 30000,\n          \"backoffMultiplier\": 2.0\n        },\n        \"circuitBreaker\": {\n          \"failureThreshold\": 5,\n          \"samplingDurationSeconds\": 30,\n          \"breakDurationSeconds\": 60\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### 4.2 Startup.cs Changes\n\n**File**: `src/UtilityChain.Node/Startup.cs`\n\n**Before** (line 101):\n```csharp\n// Infrastructure\nservices.AddSingleton<IEventBus, InMemoryEventBus>();\n```\n\n**After**:\n```csharp\n// Infrastructure - Event Bus\nvar messageBrokerConfig = ctx.Configuration.GetSection(\"services:messageBroker\");\nvar messageBrokerEnabled = messageBrokerConfig.GetValue<bool>(\"enabled\");\n\nif (messageBrokerEnabled)\n{\n    // External message broker (RabbitMQ)\n    services.Configure<MessageBrokerOptions>(messageBrokerConfig);\n    services.AddSingleton<IMessageBroker, RabbitMQMessageBroker>();\n    services.AddSingleton<IEventBus, ExternalEventBus>();\n    services.AddHostedService<OutboxRelayService>();\n\n    // Add health check for RabbitMQ\n    services.AddHealthChecks()\n            .AddRabbitMQ(sp => sp.GetRequiredService<IConnection>(),\n                         name: \"rabbitmq\",\n                         tags: new[] { \"ready\" });\n}\nelse\n{\n    // Fallback to in-memory for development/testing\n    services.AddSingleton<IEventBus, InMemoryEventBus>();\n}\n```\n\n### 4.3 New Abstractions and Implementations\n\n#### 4.3.1 IMessageBroker Interface\n\n```csharp\n// src/UtilityChain.Core/Abstractions/IMessageBroker.cs\nnamespace UtilityChain.Core.Abstractions;\n\npublic interface IMessageBroker : IAsyncDisposable\n{\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Publishes an event to the message broker.\n    /// </summary>\n    ValueTask PublishAsync<TEvent>(\n        TEvent @event,\n        string? correlationId = null,\n        CancellationToken ct = default) where TEvent : class, IEvent;\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Subscribes to events of a specific type.\n    /// </summary>\n    IDisposable Subscribe<TEvent>(\n        Func<TEvent, CancellationToken, ValueTask> handler,\n        string subscriptionId,\n        SubscriptionOptions? options = null) where TEvent : class, IEvent;\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Gets the health status of the broker connection.\n    /// </summary>\n    BrokerHealthStatus GetHealthStatus();\n}\n\npublic sealed record SubscriptionOptions\n{\n    public int PrefetchCount { get; init; } = 10;\n    public bool AutoAck { get; init; } = false;\n    public TimeSpan? AckTimeout { get; init; }\n    public string? DeadLetterQueue { get; init; }\n}\n\npublic enum BrokerHealthStatus\n{\n    Healthy,\n    Degraded,\n    Unhealthy\n}\n```\n\n#### 4.3.2 RabbitMQ Implementation (Partial)\n\n```csharp\n// src/UtilityChain.Core/Services/RabbitMQMessageBroker.cs\nnamespace UtilityChain.Core.Services;\n\npublic sealed class RabbitMQMessageBroker : IMessageBroker\n{\n    private readonly IConnection _connection;\n    private readonly IModel _publishChannel;\n    private readonly ILogger<RabbitMQMessageBroker> _logger;\n    private readonly MessageBrokerOptions _options;\n    private readonly AsyncPolicy _retryPolicy;\n    private readonly ConcurrentDictionary<string, IModel> _consumerChannels = new();\n\n    public RabbitMQMessageBroker(\n        IOptions<MessageBrokerOptions> options,\n        ILogger<RabbitMQMessageBroker> logger)\n    {\n        _options = options.Value;\n        _logger = logger;\n\n        // Build resilience policy using Polly\n        _retryPolicy = Policy\n            .Handle<BrokerUnreachableException>()\n            .Or<SocketException>()\n            .WaitAndRetryAsync(\n                _options.Resilience.RetryPolicy.MaxRetries,\n                retryAttempt => TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(\n                    Math.Min(\n                        _options.Resilience.RetryPolicy.InitialDelayMs *\n                        Math.Pow(_options.Resilience.RetryPolicy.BackoffMultiplier, retryAttempt),\n                        _options.Resilience.RetryPolicy.MaxDelayMs)),\n                (ex, timeSpan, retryCount, ctx) =>\n                {\n                    _logger.LogWarning(ex,\n                        \"RabbitMQ connection failed. Retry {RetryCount} in {Delay}ms\",\n                        retryCount, timeSpan.TotalMilliseconds);\n                });\n\n        _connection = CreateConnection();\n        _publishChannel = _connection.CreateModel();\n\n        DeclareTopology();\n    }\n\n    private IConnection CreateConnection()\n    {\n        var factory = new ConnectionFactory\n        {\n            HostName = _options.Connection.Host,\n            Port = _options.Connection.Port,\n            VirtualHost = _options.Connection.VirtualHost,\n            UserName = _options.Connection.Username,\n            Password = _options.Connection.Password,\n            RequestedHeartbeat = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(_options.Connection.HeartbeatIntervalSeconds),\n            AutomaticRecoveryEnabled = true,\n            NetworkRecoveryInterval = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(10),\n            DispatchConsumersAsync = true\n        };\n\n        return factory.CreateConnection();\n    }\n\n    private void DeclareTopology()\n    {\n        // Main exchange\n        _publishChannel.ExchangeDeclare(\n            exchange: _options.Exchange.Name,\n            type: _options.Exchange.Type,\n            durable: _options.Exchange.Durable,\n            autoDelete: _options.Exchange.AutoDelete);\n\n        // Dead letter exchange\n        _publishChannel.ExchangeDeclare(\n            exchange: _options.Queues.DeadLetterExchange,\n            type: ExchangeType.Direct,\n            durable: true);\n    }\n\n    public async ValueTask PublishAsync<TEvent>(\n        TEvent @event,\n        string? correlationId = null,\n        CancellationToken ct = default) where TEvent : class, IEvent\n    {\n        var routingKey = GetRoutingKey<TEvent>();\n        var body = JsonSerializer.SerializeToUtf8Bytes(@event);\n\n        var properties = _publishChannel.CreateBasicProperties();\n        properties.ContentType = \"application/json\";\n        properties.DeliveryMode = 2; // persistent\n        properties.MessageId = Guid.NewGuid().ToString();\n        properties.Timestamp = new AmqpTimestamp(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.ToUnixTimeSeconds());\n        properties.Type = typeof(TEvent).FullName;\n\n        if (correlationId != null)\n            properties.CorrelationId = correlationId;\n\n        await _retryPolicy.ExecuteAsync(async () =>\n        {\n            _publishChannel.BasicPublish(\n                exchange: _options.Exchange.Name,\n                routingKey: routingKey,\n                basicProperties: properties,\n                body: body);\n\n            _logger.LogDebug(\"Published {EventType} to {RoutingKey}\",\n                typeof(TEvent).Name, routingKey);\n        });\n    }\n\n    public IDisposable Subscribe<TEvent>(\n        Func<TEvent, CancellationToken, ValueTask> handler,\n        string subscriptionId,\n        SubscriptionOptions? options = null) where TEvent : class, IEvent\n    {\n        options ??= new SubscriptionOptions();\n        var queueName = $\"utilitychain.{typeof(TEvent).Name}.{subscriptionId}\";\n        var routingKey = GetRoutingKey<TEvent>();\n\n        var channel = _connection.CreateModel();\n        _consumerChannels[queueName] = channel;\n\n        // Declare queue with DLQ\n        var queueArgs = new Dictionary<string, object>\n        {\n            [\"x-dead-letter-exchange\"] = _options.Queues.DeadLetterExchange,\n            [\"x-dead-letter-routing-key\"] = $\"dlq.{queueName}\"\n        };\n\n        channel.QueueDeclare(\n            queue: queueName,\n            durable: true,\n            exclusive: false,\n            autoDelete: false,\n            arguments: queueArgs);\n\n        channel.QueueBind(queueName, _options.Exchange.Name, routingKey);\n        channel.BasicQos(0, (ushort)options.PrefetchCount, false);\n\n        var consumer = new AsyncEventingBasicConsumer(channel);\n        consumer.Received += async (_, ea) =>\n        {\n            try\n            {\n                var @event = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<TEvent>(ea.Body.Span)!;\n                await handler(@event, CancellationToken.None);\n                channel.BasicAck(ea.DeliveryTag, false);\n            }\n            catch (Exception ex)\n            {\n                _logger.LogError(ex, \"Error processing {EventType}\", typeof(TEvent).Name);\n                channel.BasicNack(ea.DeliveryTag, false, false); // Send to DLQ\n            }\n        };\n\n        channel.BasicConsume(queueName, false, consumer);\n\n        return new SubscriptionHandle(() =>\n        {\n            channel.Close();\n            _consumerChannels.TryRemove(queueName, out _);\n        });\n    }\n\n    private static string GetRoutingKey<TEvent>() => typeof(TEvent).FullName!.ToLowerInvariant();\n\n    public BrokerHealthStatus GetHealthStatus()\n    {\n        return _connection.IsOpen ? BrokerHealthStatus.Healthy : BrokerHealthStatus.Unhealthy;\n    }\n\n    public async ValueTask DisposeAsync()\n    {\n        foreach (var channel in _consumerChannels.Values)\n            channel.Close();\n\n        _publishChannel.Close();\n        _connection.Close();\n    }\n\n    private sealed class SubscriptionHandle : IDisposable\n    {\n        private readonly Action _disposeAction;\n        public SubscriptionHandle(Action disposeAction) => _disposeAction = disposeAction;\n        public void Dispose() => _disposeAction();\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Resilience and Error Handling Strategy\n\n### 5.1 New Failure Modes Introduced\n\n| Failure Mode | Description | Mitigation |\n|--------------|-------------|------------|\n| **Network Partition** | Node loses connectivity to broker | Circuit breaker + local buffering |\n| **Broker Downtime** | RabbitMQ cluster unavailable | Outbox pattern ensures eventual delivery |\n| **Poison Messages** | Messages that repeatedly fail processing | Dead Letter Queue + manual intervention |\n| **Consumer Lag** | Subscribers can't keep up with producers | Backpressure, scaling consumers |\n| **Message Expiry** | TTL exceeded before processing | Alerting, DLQ inspection |\n| **Duplicate Delivery** | At-least-once semantics can cause duplicates | Idempotent handlers (deduplication) |\n\n### 5.2 Dead Letter Queue (DLQ) Strategy\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                    DLQ PROCESSING FLOW                          │\n├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│                                                                 │\n│  Normal Queue                                                   │\n│      │                                                          │\n│      ├──▶ Handler Success ──▶ ACK ──▶ Remove from Queue        │\n│      │                                                          │\n│      └──▶ Handler Failure ──▶ NACK ──▶ DLQ                     │\n│                                         │                       │\n│                                         ▼                       │\n│                               ┌─────────────────┐              │\n│                               │  Dead Letter    │              │\n│                               │  Queue (DLQ)    │              │\n│                               └────────┬────────┘              │\n│                                        │                        │\n│                     ┌──────────────────┼──────────────────┐    │\n│                     ▼                  ▼                  ▼    │\n│              ┌──────────┐      ┌──────────┐      ┌──────────┐ │\n│              │ Alert    │      │ Manual   │      │ Requeue  │ │\n│              │ Ops Team │      │ Inspect  │      │ w/ Delay │ │\n│              └──────────┘      └──────────┘      └──────────┘ │\n│                                                                 │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### 5.3 Retry Policy Configuration (Polly)\n\n```csharp\n// Applied to both publishing and consuming\nservices.AddSingleton<IAsyncPolicy>(sp =>\n{\n    var options = sp.GetRequiredService<IOptions<MessageBrokerOptions>>().Value;\n\n    var retryPolicy = Policy\n        .Handle<BrokerUnreachableException>()\n        .Or<AlreadyClosedException>()\n        .WaitAndRetryAsync(\n            options.Resilience.RetryPolicy.MaxRetries,\n            retryAttempt => TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(\n                options.Resilience.RetryPolicy.InitialDelayMs *\n                Math.Pow(options.Resilience.RetryPolicy.BackoffMultiplier, retryAttempt)));\n\n    var circuitBreaker = Policy\n        .Handle<BrokerUnreachableException>()\n        .CircuitBreakerAsync(\n            exceptionsAllowedBeforeBreaking: options.Resilience.CircuitBreaker.FailureThreshold,\n            durationOfBreak: TimeSpan.FromSeconds(options.Resilience.CircuitBreaker.BreakDurationSeconds),\n            onBreak: (ex, duration) =>\n                Log.Warning(\"Circuit breaker opened for {Duration}s due to {Exception}\",\n                           duration.TotalSeconds, ex.GetType().Name),\n            onReset: () => Log.Information(\"Circuit breaker reset\"),\n            onHalfOpen: () => Log.Information(\"Circuit breaker half-open\"));\n\n    return Policy.WrapAsync(retryPolicy, circuitBreaker);\n});\n```\n\n### 5.4 Idempotent Handler Pattern\n\nTo handle duplicate message delivery:\n\n```csharp\npublic class IdempotentEventHandler<TEvent> : IEventHandler<TEvent>\n    where TEvent : class, IEvent\n{\n    private readonly IEventHandler<TEvent> _inner;\n    private readonly IProcessedEventStore _processedStore;\n    private readonly ILogger _logger;\n\n    public async ValueTask HandleAsync(TEvent @event, CancellationToken ct)\n    {\n        var eventId = GetEventId(@event);\n\n        if (await _processedStore.HasBeenProcessedAsync(eventId, ct))\n        {\n            _logger.LogDebug(\"Event {EventId} already processed, skipping\", eventId);\n            return;\n        }\n\n        await _inner.HandleAsync(@event, ct);\n        await _processedStore.MarkAsProcessedAsync(eventId, ct);\n    }\n\n    private static string GetEventId(TEvent @event) =>\n        @event switch\n        {\n            IHasEventId e => e.EventId.ToString(),\n            _ => @event.GetHashCode().ToString()\n        };\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6. Architectural Trade-offs Analysis\n\n### 6.1 Advantages (Pros)\n\n| Benefit | Description | Impact |\n|---------|-------------|--------|\n| **Horizontal Scalability** | Services can be scaled independently; multiple consumers can share workload | High |\n| **Temporal Decoupling** | Publishers and subscribers don't need to be online simultaneously | High |\n| **Durability** | Messages persist across restarts; no data loss on crashes | Critical |\n| **Guaranteed Delivery** | At-least-once semantics with acknowledgments | High |\n| **Fault Isolation** | Broker failure doesn't crash the node; local buffering via outbox | High |\n| **Observability** | Message broker provides metrics, tracing, queue depths | Medium |\n| **Independent Deployments** | Services can be updated without coordinated deployments | Medium |\n| **Load Leveling** | Spiky workloads absorbed by queue; consumers process at their own pace | Medium |\n\n### 6.2 Disadvantages (Cons)\n\n| Drawback | Description | Mitigation |\n|----------|-------------|------------|\n| **Operational Complexity** | Additional infrastructure (RabbitMQ cluster) to manage | Use managed service (CloudAMQP, Amazon MQ) |\n| **Increased Latency** | Network hop + serialization adds ~1-10ms per event | Acceptable for most use cases; keep in-memory for latency-critical paths |\n| **Eventual Consistency** | Subscribers see state changes with delay | Design for eventual consistency; use compensating transactions |\n| **Debugging Difficulty** | Distributed tracing required; harder to follow event flow | Implement correlation IDs, structured logging, distributed tracing (Jaeger) |\n| **Message Ordering** | Global ordering not guaranteed across partitions | Use single partition for strict ordering; design for out-of-order delivery |\n| **Duplicate Handling** | At-least-once semantics requires idempotent handlers | Implement deduplication at handler level |\n| **Infrastructure Cost** | Additional servers, storage, network | Cost justified by resilience gains |\n| **Schema Evolution** | Event payload changes require versioning strategy | Use schema registry; forward-compatible serialization |\n\n### 6.3 Risk Assessment Matrix\n\n| Risk | Probability | Impact | Mitigation |\n|------|-------------|--------|------------|\n| Broker cluster failure | Low | Critical | Multi-AZ deployment, automatic failover |\n| Message backlog overflow | Medium | High | Queue size limits, alerting, backpressure |\n| Poison message loops | Medium | Medium | DLQ with TTL, manual inspection |\n| Schema incompatibility | Medium | High | Versioned schemas, compatibility testing |\n| Network latency spikes | Medium | Medium | Timeouts, circuit breakers |\n\n---\n\n## 7. Migration Plan\n\n### 7.1 Phased Rollout\n\n| Phase | Description | Duration | Rollback Plan |\n|-------|-------------|----------|---------------|\n| **Phase 1** | Deploy RabbitMQ cluster; add configuration | 1 week | Remove config |\n| **Phase 2** | Implement dual-write (in-memory + outbox) | 2 weeks | Disable outbox |\n| **Phase 3** | Deploy outbox relay service | 1 week | Stop relay service |\n| **Phase 4** | Migrate non-critical events (telemetry, logging) | 2 weeks | Feature flag |\n| **Phase 5** | Migrate critical events (consensus, transactions) | 3 weeks | Feature flag |\n| **Phase 6** | Remove in-memory bus; full external | 1 week | Re-enable in-memory |\n\n### 7.2 Feature Flags\n\n```json\n{\n  \"featureFlags\": {\n    \"useExternalBroker\": false,\n    \"externalBrokerEvents\": [\n      \"TransactionAcceptedEvent\",\n      \"TransactionRejectedEvent\"\n    ],\n    \"dualWriteEnabled\": true\n  }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 8. Conclusion\n\nThe migration from `InMemoryEventBus` to an external message broker with the Transactional Outbox pattern addresses critical scalability and resilience limitations in the UtilityChain Core Suite. While introducing operational complexity, the benefits—particularly guaranteed delivery, temporal decoupling, and horizontal scalability—are essential for mainnet readiness with high transaction volumes and complex DeFi protocols.\n\n**Recommended Next Steps**:\n1. Approve this design proposal\n2. Provision RabbitMQ cluster in staging environment\n3. Implement IMessageBroker abstraction and RabbitMQ adapter\n4. Add outbox table to database schema\n5. Modify TransactionProcessor as proof-of-concept\n6. Execute phased migration plan\n\n---\n\n## Appendix A: Files Requiring Modification\n\n| File | Change Type | Description |\n|------|-------------|-------------|\n| `config/node-config.default.json` | Add | `messageBroker` configuration section |\n| `src/UtilityChain.Node/Startup.cs:101` | Modify | Conditional IEventBus registration |\n| `src/UtilityChain.Core/Abstractions/IMessageBroker.cs` | New | Message broker interface |\n| `src/UtilityChain.Core/Services/RabbitMQMessageBroker.cs` | New | RabbitMQ implementation |\n| `src/UtilityChain.Core/Services/ExternalEventBus.cs` | New | IEventBus A background relay service polls the outbox and publishes to the external broker\n\n4. **Configuration Changes**:\n   - New `messageBroker` section in `node-config.default.json`\n   - Conditional registration in `Startup.cs` based on configuration\n   - Circuit breaker pattern for broker failures\n   - Idempotent handler pattern for duplicate message handling\n\n6. **Trade-offs Analysis**:\n   - **Pros**: Scalability, durability, temporal decoupling, guaranteed delivery, fault isolation\n   -
